# PSWP Register Schema HISTORY (reconstructed, 9-Sep-2026, v126 workbook)

**Read this first.** The original `PSWP_Register_Schema_HISTORY.md`, described in the lean
schema as 429 sections and roughly 243 KB covering v10 to v122, was not in the session zip and
could not be recovered. This file is a RECONSTRUCTION assembled from the workbook itself, which
rule 14 makes the self-contained record: the Handover sheet, the Method sheet, the
Data_Acquisition register, the Vendor_Series footnotes and the versioned Config keys. It is not
the original document and does not claim to be. Where the original carried prose reasoning that
was never written into the workbook, that reasoning is gone.

It ships in the session zip and must NOT be placed in project knowledge, where it would be
loaded on every turn at no benefit (rule 19.1).

Sections below are verbatim from the workbook. Nothing is paraphrased, and nothing is added.

---

## 1. Handover, every change note in the workbook

The Handover sheet is the workbook's own change log. 132 rows.

## 2. Method, the stated method as built

The Method sheet carries the numbered method sections. 326 rows.

- PS & WP Transaction Register, 3-FY build (v10; scope widened v118)
- Scope: FY2023/24 and FY2024/25 at service code 20392 (both sections); FY2025/26 section-wide, all 19 service codes, both sections (from v118); FY2026/27 memo year, section-wide, P1-3 posted, both sections (from v118). Rule 11 asymmetry between the first two years and the third is documented at Open Item #415.
- 1.0 Scope note | The three loaded years are NOT the same scope. FY2026/27 P1 covers both full sections. FY2023/24 and FY2024/25 cover header PK000022 only, periods 1 to 12 (period 13 excluded from the exports, so EOY accrual postings sit asymmetrically across year boundaries). Cross-year comparison is valid only at PK000022, gross, T9 excluded, and remains provisional until period 13 and FY2025/26 (26SLACT) are loaded. [CORRECTED v112: TechOne has no period 13. The FY2025/26 End of Year accrual posts in period 12 on PJ009910; no period-13 layer exists and none was ever missing.]
- 2.0 Rules of record | This workbook applies the standing rules in PSWP_Project_Instructions.md: Work Order sub-PK, per-line nature (never per vendor), full capture of every source column, Theme_Map governance, T9 quarantine, journal pairing, ex-GST amounts, ABR-anchored contractor identification, unique LineKeys, control totals to the cent.
- 3.0 Prior-year contractor identification | The LEV002 full creditor history (22,447 invoices, Jul-2017 to Jul-2026) anchors T & H Levai across all years at Tier 1. Other prior-year suppliers are series-level unidentified pending their creditor histories; the garbage series is attributed to Cleanaway on the strength of the Cleanaway-named invoices and journal narrations in the same monthly series, flagged series-inferred. Prior-year generic "STANDING ORDER" narrations ($2.2M FY2023/24, $1.9M FY2024/25) carry account-based category inference only.
- 4.0 Themes v2 | Taxonomy expanded from 9 to 13 themes on the prior-year evidence, exactly as anticipated: T10 Waste collection, T11 Plumbing & water assets, T12 Security & access, T13 Recoveries claims & offsets. All years are stated on the v2 taxonomy. Edit Theme_Map to split further; restate all years when you do.
- 5.0 Waste accrual churn | The garbage account runs a monthly accrue/reverse/actual journal cycle (Parks EOM Accrue-Cleanaway). Gross waste-theme dollars therefore overstate cash activity within a year; the cycle nets. Read the annual waste figure net, unlike other themes.
- 6.0 Rule 17: what Confirmed means | From 30-Jul-2026, Confirmed requires two things together: the invoice has been read, and every printed detail on it has been recorded on the register line that carries it. Line capture on Evidence_Invoice_Lines is necessary but no longer sufficient. The register therefore carries a sighted-invoice evidence block (green, columns CJ to DW) holding the vendor, ABN, ACN, address, phone, invoice and due dates, PO, contract, bill-to and ship-to, requesting officer, request date and channel, CR/WO number, PK as printed and normalised, site details and contact, work description, technician, line count, the line items and the works narrative verbatim, printed subtotal, GST, total, payments and balance, payment details, terms, pages, and three live checks. The block sits after the grey source block because inserting columns would break the live cross-sheet formulas. Status and Verdict stay independent: a line can be Confirmed on evidence and still carry a Review verdict on coding, and Confirmed does not assert that every question is answered, only that everything printed has been captured.
- 7.0 Accrual pairing on the Harpley series | All five invoices in the 000151xx-000152xx run pair 1:1 with FY2025/26 EOY accrual reversals under GJ080271, same PK and same 73212 account: $31,223.34, $12,914.55, $9,016.15, $2,526.50 and $1,963.89. The net effect on FY2026/27 P1 is nil and the cost belongs to FY2025/26. Read the gross P1 figures on this series as accrual churn, not activity, per rule 5.
- 8.0 Rule 17 staging | The seven pre-v4 invoices are FY2025/26-dated and have no register line in the loaded scope, so their register evidence blocks cannot exist yet. Their complete printed field set is staged on Evidence_Invoices (columns N to AF), verbatim from Binder 1, so the workbook stands alone (rule 14) and the source can be discarded (rule 15) without dropping any printed information (rule 3). On the 26SLACT load the staged fields transfer to the green block (CJ to DW) of each ledger line, the three per-line checks go live, and Confirmed becomes available, in the same session, before that load ships. The binder re-supply was also used as a verbatim audit: six Evidence_Invoice_Lines captures were corrected to the printed form (spacing and hyphenation, plus one unprinted month removed), each with the correction noted in its Notes cell, and two staging controls were added (blocks held 7 of 7; line reconciliations TRUE 9 of 9).
- 9.0 LineKey repair (v9) | Rule 9 scopes the sequence within (doc, account, PK), with blank Document Unique IDs taking the NODOC token. The v8 build numbered NODOC sequences per source load, so 11 FY2024/25 recode-journal rows (RJ012111, RJ012112) collided with FY2023/24 keys (RJ011823, RJ011824, RJ011904). v9 renumbers the 11 later rows to continue each (account, PK) sequence in load order. No other sheet references these keys; amounts, references and formulas are untouched; uniqueness now asserts TRUE across all 14,002 lines and stays part of the every-load assertion.
- 10.0 Session efficiency standard (v10) | Sessions from v10 run instructions v5 rule 18: a single accumulating build script per version, one recalculation, one verification pass; capture content held as structured data and applied by a generic writer; duplicate uploads identified by printed-subtotal and hash comparison before any full-text re-read; unchanged sheets verified by checksum. Third-party extractions (e.g. Copilot) are accepted as raw text with page breaks preserved, logged in Data_Acquisition with a spot-check result; rules 16 and 17 reconciliation always runs here against the raw text. The 26SLACT session applies this standard: the staged rows on Evidence_Invoices N to AF transfer to register green blocks by a mechanical keyed loop, not re-composition.
- 11.0 v11 batch capture and check variants | 144 invoices captured 30-Jul-2026 from three third-party transcription batches (tool unstated; hashes in Data_Acquisition). Rule 17 check variants introduced, each flagged "[check variant]" in the anomalies column: (a) one-cent tolerance on check 2 where the GL ex-GST is a TechOne derivation of the GST-inclusive actual (six Pool Shop lines, rule 7); (b) derivation equality on check 2 where the register equals the printed incl-GST total divided by 1.1 (Hilltops, Bethania); (c) split-posting check 2 tying each register line to its own captured invoice lines by LineKey (Urban Fountains 34629, one invoice, three lines); (d) GST-free acceptance on check 3 (Hilltops, tax code FRE, printed Tax $0.00); (e) two-cent per-line-rounding tolerance on check 3 (Bethania); (f) template-B Harpley invoices print line amounts GST-inclusive, so check 1 ties to the printed Total Inc GST and the evidence-header control allows $16,554.08 of template-B GST. These variants are proposed conventions for ratification in the next instructions revision. Scope note: prior years remain PK000022-only; the FY2025/26 sighted invoices (seven staged pre-v4) remain pending the 26SLACT load.

- Check-formula variants ratified v18 (Method 11.0 extended; instructions v10 to restate in rule 17): (i) 123 group - one printed collective sub-account row, N register component lines; =IF(ROUND(SUMIF($CJ$5:$CJ$19232,CJ{r},$T$5:$T$19232),2)=DI{r},...): the register group sum by Ev Invoice ID ties to the printed (derived) subtotal. (ii) 124 grossgst - printed GST equals 10% of gross charges before GST-neutral (FRE) pay-on-time discounts; =IF(DJ{r}=ROUND((DI{r}-SUMIFS(EIL G where A=CJ{r} and G<0))*0.1,2),...). (iii) 124 tolNc-collective - GST tolerance of 0.5c per register component line on collective sub-accounts (=...<=ROUND(COUNTIF($CJ$5:$CJ$19232,CJ{r})*0.005,2)); one application (70561309, 3c on 8 lines, Verdict Review). Every variant row carries the [check variant] tag (control >= 855; actual 802 new tags this build).

EI data 5:1677 (totals 1679, correspondence 1681-1682, controls 1685:1698); EIL 5:3915; recon 3919:5591 (1,673 rows); both citation sets rewritten from the EvID map.
- 32.1 Check-2 partial-scope variant (NEW, ratified this build; fold into instructions v10). Where a sighted invoice is Council-wide and the loaded PS/WP scope carries only a SUBSET of its printed lines - the balance posting outside 4090240/4090260 and therefore invisible to this register - check 2 ties the register amount to that row’s own captured line(s) by LineKey, using the ratified LineKey split-posting formula; checks 1 and 3 continue to tie the whole printed invoice. Distinct from the split-posting variant (all sibling lines visible in the register) and from the sum-tie variant (several register lines against one printed line). The association must be FIXED by evidence, never inferred: precedent CMBM SI-047499, where the GL narration "Berrinba Wetlands" plus the amount identify exactly one of 159 printed lines. Where no such evidence fixes the line, the row is HELD, not green-blocked. Tagged [check variant]; tag floor 869 -> 872.
- 32.2 Defect family F11, column-header token bleed (NEW). On column-ruled templates the wrapped tail of a column header can attach to the first captured description ("Line Amount Excl. GST" -> "GST Animal Management Centre ..."). Detected by a leading header token on line 1 only; repaired from the retained page text with the basis stated in the anomalies field. Observed once (CMBM SI-047499). pswp_json_repair.py doc block updated.
- 32.4 Citation-drift repair, register-row limb (NEW). Evidence_Invoices col 13 carries TWO citations: the reconciliation row on Evidence_Invoice_Lines and the register row(s) on which the rule 17 checks live. Successive builds rewrote the recon limb with a blanket re.sub(r"row \d+"), which also overwrote the register limb with the recon-row number - 141 of the 285 register-row clauses were wrong at v31. Both limbs are now rewritten from authoritative maps (EvID -> recon row; EvID -> the register rows carrying that green block, read from column CJ), never by positional shift, and the verify pass audits BOTH permanently. Invoices with no register match cite "(no register match)".
- 32.3 Header restatement from retained page text (rule 19.2). The b29 corpus carried amounts, lines and a partial header set only. The rule 17 header fields (vendor address, phone/email, bill-to, ship-to, PO, contract, requesting officer, site, CR/WO, payments made, balance due, licence) were restated per vendor template from the retained verbatim page text, and EVERY literal token was asserted present in that invoice’s own page text before writing (0 failures). Two letterheads print address blocks in side-by-side columns that the text layer interleaves (CMBM, Elemental); those blocks were de-interleaved and the reconstruction is stated in the anomalies field with its basis. Fields the layout does not carry stay "(not printed)".

A disagreement is a finding, never a silent re-capture. Traffex 180246 counts 4 EI lines (one zero-amount descriptor row captured at v31) against 3 priced lines in the corpus - agreed on the priced set.

- 34.1 Defect family F12 (NEW): wrapped description tail. Where a printed description is wider than the description column, the tail prints on the NEXT layout row, which the extractor classifies as NARRATIVE and drops - so the captured description truncates mid-phrase ("Routine Inspection and removal of debris from"). Repair only where the tail is the immediately adjacent narrative line, carries no amount, carries no label prefix and runs to six words or fewer; restate by joining, and state the basis in the anomalies field. Observed on 8 of 68 lines; the same description prints un-wrapped on the 6 Xero-layout lines, which cross-checks the repair to the word.
- 34.2 Shared Xero reference across vendors (NEW method note): Xero INV-# references collide across vendors and years (schema section 9). The rule 12 EvID screen must therefore distinguish a re-sighting from a COLLISION - the same id belonging to a different supplier. Batch 31 hit one: INV-3412 is both a Pool Shop QLD invoice ($31,142.16, 26-Jul-2023, register row 210, captured earlier) and a Play Force Australia invoice ($1,142.28, 19-May-2025, register row 1563). The collision is resolved by an EvID override in the brief (PF-3412); the register Reference is left as posted on both rows and target selection is by printed subtotal, never by reference alone.
- 34.3 Driver extension (rule 20): pswp_build_batch.py gains evid_overrides (invoice id -> EvID, for shared-reference collisions) and bp_overrides (invoice id -> payment/terms boilerplate keys, single or list). One vendor can print several payment blocks across a series or an entity change - Play Force prints three (BP:INV-P3 old entity, BP:INV-P9 new entity classic layout, BP:INV-P22 new entity Xero layout, minted here) and two terms notices, so a single per-family key could not carry the evidence. Both maps are validated against Vendor_Boilerplate and recorded in the build summary.

Writes are identification only: creditor code, contractor, ABN, enquiry narration, enquiry block, Nature Basis "Creditor history (APLEDGER)", Evidence Tier 1, evidence citation naming the Creditor_Lines row, Status Pending evidence -> Partial. A green-blocked row is never an identification target.

A creditor-history reference match is accepted ONLY where the creditor transaction amount, which is INCL GST, equals the register line ex GST at 1.1 within 2c, or the register line is a $0.00 companion. Batch 32 held 120 matches back on this test and Open Item 89 proves them to be a second vendor (Q Power, ABN 82 067 507 591, invoice 14150 sighted in the same upload).

- 40.1 RATIFIED: check-1 per-line rounding tolerance (Method 40.1; fold into instructions rule 17). Where a vendor prints each line amount rounded to two decimals but computes the printed subtotal from the UNROUNDED values, the captured lines cannot equal the printed subtotal and the printed document does not self-tie. Check 1 then takes the standard tolerance form =IF(ROUND(ABS(DQ{r}-DI{r}),2)<=0.01,"TRUE","FALSE"), tagged [check variant], with the delta and a worked example stated in the anomalies field. This is the same species as the already-ratified check-2 one-cent tolerance (TechOne ex-GST derivation) and check-3 rounding tolerances, and completes the set across all three checks. Precedent and first use: Play Force Australia INV-4412, INV-6739, INV-7609 and INV-7759 (5.50 x $105.41 = $579.755, printed $579.75), held uncaptured at v40 and released here. The reconciliation rows for these four take the ratified tolerance E-form. VARIANT_TAG_MIN 876 -> 880.
- 40.2 Open Item #107 CLOSED - recovering an invoice the extractor never emitted. BLC 001291 ($885.00, 9-May-2025, Windaroo Lake Park wire balustrade reinstatement, CR 3682017, PO 707879) was absent from the Batch 37 corpus because document segmentation merged it into record 001290 (Binder111.pdf pages 13-16, of which 15-16 are this separate invoice). It was recovered from the retained page text of that merged record. The page-15 text layer inserts spurious spaces inside words ("L o g a n City Council", "Invoice n u m b e r : 001 291"), so every field was de-spaced and then cross-checked field by field against the eleven clean sibling BLC invoices captured at v40, which share the template and boilerplate verbatim; the printed figures carry no spacing artefact and were read directly. The basis is stated in the anomalies field per rule 16a: this is reconstruction of a defective text layer, not paraphrase. ROUTE: where a text layer is character-spaced but structurally intact, de-space and cross-check against a clean sibling of the same template rather than discarding the capture.
- 40.3 Levai template and identities. The Levai (Xero) layout prints a DESCRIPTION / AMOUNT table with NO qty and NO unit-price column, so the extractor classified every line NARRATIVE and four documents captured $0.00 - the whole invoice lost, not a line (defect family F9, now seen on five documents across five vendors in two batches; it is the single highest-value fix for extraction prompt v3). The Levai letterhead address changes from "Lot 2 Industrial Ave" to "41 Industrial Ave" between May-2024 and Jun-2024 under the same ABN, so the address is parsed per invoice. Two new Tier 1 identities: 4Park Pty Ltd (56 657 333 296, trading as Forpark, WA) and TradeTools Pty Ltd (67 010 700 053), both previously carried as Unidentified series labels on account 72111. The 4Park printed Amount column is GST-INCLUSIVE and its extractor record lumped both printed lines into one $2,940.00 summary row; the TradeTools line amount was attached to a footer warranty clause. Both restored to the printed line tables (rule 16, a summary row in place of line capture is a critical failure).
- 40.4 Findings: T & H Levai and Play Force Australia print the SAME premises, 41 Industrial Ave Logan Village, with adjacent landlines ((07) 3803 0032 and (07) 3803 1788) across 123 and 118 sighted green blocks respectively, on different ABNs - a related-party question for Finance (#113). Two 2019-vintage Levai invoices (00021470 $918.00 and 00022061 $2,775.00, the latter on PK000391) sit in the binder outside the register scope with no register line and are NOT captured; 00021470 has no text layer at all (#114). INV-36682 and INV-36683 bill an identical $1,520.00 on the same day and are confirmed as distinct jobs from their printed job lines (#115). Two Levai target rows were legacy Confirmed on the narration with no rule 17 evidence (the Open Items #95 pattern) and are completed here (#116).
- 41.0 Site dimension (NEW v42). Cost per park / site is now a first-class register dimension. Column DY carries the normalised park or site name and column DZ the basis on which it was attributed. Nothing is inferred: a line is attributed only where a source field carries a FULL PARK NAME matching the gazetteer (user directive, 18-Aug-2026). Sources are read in evidence order - rates parcel narration, sighted invoice site details (printed), sighted invoice work description, electricity supply point, GL narration, enquiry narration - and the first hit wins, with the source recorded as the basis.
- 41.1 The gazetteer. 646 park names. The primary source is the internal-rates S07 narration series, which prints parcel id, street address and park name (1,139 parcels, 583 carrying a park name); these give properly cased, authoritative Logan park names with their addresses. A secondary harvest takes any capitalised multi-word name ending Park / Reserve / Gardens / Wetlands / Forest / Parklands / Oval / Bushland / Green / Square / Common that recurs three or more times across site details, work descriptions and supply points. Generic constructions are blocklisted (General Park, Skate Park, Dog Park, Water Park, Car Park, Memorial Park, Sports Park, District Park, Adventure Park and the like) because they name a facility type, not a site; 'General Park maintenance' had otherwise attributed 152 lines and $72,856 to a park that does not exist.
- 41.2 Multi-site lines are HELD, never split. 319 lines ($904,116.12) name several parks on one line - a Council-wide grounds contract billing a schedule of sites, a standing order across a maintenance round. Column DY carries the semicolon-joined list of every park named and column DZ reads 'Multiple sites named (not allocated)'. No allocation is made, because no evidence fixes the split; this is the v32 partial-scope principle applied to the site dimension. Allocating them on a head-count or dollar-weighted basis would be fabrication. Precedent: register row 14039 ($33,335.10, PAR/340E/2026) prints 'Logan Gardens; Flagstone; Darlington Parklands; ...' and an earlier single-park pass had attributed the whole amount to one of them.
- 41.3 Coverage at v42. 5452 lines ($2,407,766.77, 21.2% of the register) attributed to 483 named parks; 319 lines ($904,116.12, 7.9%) held multi-site; 15698 lines ($8,065,429.62, 70.9%) carry no park name in any source field. The unattributed majority is not a defect: most Parks spend is coded at PK level against a maintenance round or a whole-of-city contract and never names a site. Coverage rises with sighted-invoice capture, which is the only source that reliably prints a site.
- 41.4 Batch 39 reconciliations (analysis, no register write). FY2025/26 public place bins: all twelve monthly Parks postings equal the Waste branch driver (LCC DOCS-19541843-v1, row Parks, coded (PK) PK000022-7B115) to the cent, $1,123,128.88. FY2025/26 electricity: 746 register lines on the five uploaded Origin statements group to 234 sites by NMI and every one ties register ex GST to the Origin statement site total to the cent, $27,621.35. Both worked in PSWP_Batch39_Waste_and_Energy_Analysis.xlsx.
- 42.0 Themes restated (v43). The sheet had accumulated three FALSE-reading controls: a grand-total test frozen at $10,677,585.33 (v14), an FY2026/27 P1 test frozen at $139,603.35, and a header still reading 'three loaded years' with FY2025/26 bolted on as a ninth column. Restated as four FY columns in year order plus an all-years column, PK000022 mirrored, and six controls that read the register live: three prior-year extracts (frozen, correct), FY2026/27 = register minus those three (live, part-year), all years = Register!$T$21475, PK000022 = SUMIFS. A data-quality block lists FY (Service) values outside the loaded years. Standing rule: a control that tests a frozen figure for a part-year scope will go FALSE on the next load; part-year controls tie live.
- 42.1 Site basis is exhaustive (v43). Column DZ now carries one of: a park attribution source, 'Multiple sites named (not allocated)', or a closed-list class - 'Non-park Council facility (ship-to = Parks Depot)', 'City-wide network service (no site by design)', 'Accounting event (no site by construction)', 'Internal charge (not site-specific)', 'Invoice sighted, printed site not in gazetteer (candidate harvest)', 'Supply-point address only (address route pending)', 'No invoice sighted (awaiting capture)', 'No site determinable'. Every group is COUNTIF/SUMIF-able on the Sites sheet and the tie check remains exhaustive. The unattributed majority is now explained by class rather than left blank: at v43 108 lines / $3.07M are a whole-of-city network service, 8627 lines / $2.67M await invoice capture, 2566 lines are accounting events. A park attribution to any of these would be fabrication.
- 42.2 Whole-row source hierarchy (v43). The v42 build read six source fields. A scan of all 130 register columns on the 15,698 unattributed rows found park names in columns never read: 111 Ev Line items (printed) - 92 lines / $845,336 - and 112 Ev Works carried out (printed) - 87 lines / $416,278 - together 92% of the recoverable names; also 99 Ev Ship To (21 lines, all the Parks Depot), 80 Src User Fld 2, 127 Ev Anomalies. Order is now: 111 > 106 > 112 > 108 > 127 > 99 (depot only) > 42 > 22 > 23 > 80, first hit wins, source recorded as the basis. Six columns are BANNED as sources: 12 Contractor and 32 Coding Note (own analysis, circular), 25 Nature Detail (derived from the sighted lines already read at 111), 92 Ev Vendor address (supplier premises - 20 false hits on '15 Helios St, Shailer Park'), 98 Ev Bill To (Council's address), 105 Ev PK normalised. Standing rule: a supplier's premises, Council's billing address and Claude's own labels are never a site source.
- 42.3 Multi-site holds extended (v43). 408 lines now held (v42 319). Precedent from the new sources: the Pool Shop QLD monthly standing-order invoices print 'Alexander Park; Darlington Parklands; Logan Gardens' in the line items field on every invoice, so all fifteen ($394,264.81) are held as multi-site rather than attributed to PK000022's header. Ship-to = Parks Depot is a non-park Council facility (21 lines) and is classed, not attributed. Two blocklist additions: the label 'Undertake General Park' (a facility-type construction that had leaked into Panel A at v42) is removed from the gazetteer; harvest prefixes Mobile / Storm / Maintenance / Supply / Install are blocked.
- 42.4 Verification of the new writes (v43). The 194 park writes made from the newly read columns are exact gazetteer-name matches (the same evidence class the v42 build wrote from col 106) and are listed line by line in b43_site_writes_for_verification.json (register row, LineKey, amount, site, basis, source column, source text). Open Items #129 holds them for the user's check; any that fail revert to 'Invoice sighted, printed site not in gazetteer (candidate harvest)' at the next build. The 45 gazetteer candidates harvested from the 'Invoice sighted, printed site not in gazetteer (candidate harvest)' class (Quandong Park $30,255, Horseshoe Park $21,950, Lavell Park -> Lavelle Park, Tygum Skate Park -> Tygum Park, and others) ship in PSWP_Site_Attribution_Worklist_v42_fullscan.xlsx for adjudication and are NOT written.
- 42.5 Address route deferred (v43). 3075 lines / $117,089 carry a supply-point street address and no park name (Origin Energy dominates, 3,026 lines). A street-name join to the Parks Directory was tested and REJECTED: 26 directory streets serve two or more parks (Underwood Rd four, Paradise Rd four), and OpenStreetMap geocoding returns the road centre with no house-number precision, so a 200 m point-in-polygon test disagreed with the directory on the three largest supply points. The route reopens on property-level coordinates only: G-NAF address points (QLD_ADDRESS_DEFAULT_GEOCODE) or Council's own cadastre keyed on Property_Key, with point-in-polygon against the LoganHub Existing parks layer at zero buffer and every hit flagged for user check.
- 43.0 Site read at printed-line level (v44). The v43 build read the site from the register row's line-items field (col 111), which prints EVERY line of the invoice, and so held any invoice naming several parks. But Evidence_Invoice_Lines already carries each printed line with its own amount, printed site and printed PK (rule 16). Where each printed line names one park, the invoice's split across sites is FIXED BY EVIDENCE and is written, not held: 128 register rows explode to 628 (LineKey, park) rows on Site_Allocation, $1,187,581.57, every LineKey tying to Register!T to the cent by construction (residue row = register amount less the park-named lines). Precedent: Pool Shop QLD INV-5278, one register line $37,850.00 on PK000022, fifteen printed lines - Logan Gardens Water Play $5,512.58 PK000469, Flagstone Water Play $6,309.56 PK000470, Darlington Parklands $4,836.00 PK000441, Beenleigh Water Play $3,334.82 PK000419, Forest Glen Water Feature $4,270.36. The Water Parks section becomes readable per site for the first time (Method 9 PoolShop cross-posting). Holding these at v43 was over-caution, not rigour: the v32 partial-scope principle says write where evidence fixes the line.
- 43.1 Two-level site model (v44). Level 1, Evidence_Invoice_Lines cols L/M: site per printed line, basis 'Printed line names one park' / 'several parks (held)' / 'No park named'. Level 2, Register DY/DZ: single-site rows unchanged; multi-site rows with tied printed lines read 'Multiple sites named (allocated per printed line, see Site_Allocation)' and their dollars sit on Site_Allocation; multi-site rows with no tied printed lines stay 'Multiple sites named (not allocated)'. Sites Panel A = SUMIF(Register DY) + SUMIF(Site_Allocation G:H) per park; a group row carries the allocation residue; the tie check stays exhaustive.
- 43.2 Electricity supply points reconciled to site by Council address records (v44). Three internal address indexes: the S07 internal-rates parcel narrations (parcel id, street, park name - Council's own record), the Parks Strategy Layer House_Add, and the Parks Directory address. Rule: number+street exact and unique across the three -> written (420 lines, basis 'Electricity supply point (address = Council parcel record, number and street exact)'); street unique but no number match -> written AND flagged (582 lines, basis 'Electricity supply point (address = Council parcel record, street unique) [USER CHECK]', own group row on Sites, listed line by line for the user); street serving several parks, or not in any Council record -> stays 'Supply-point address only (address route pending)' (2073 lines; 99 Ewing Rd Woodridge alone is 217 lines and resolves to Booran / Rainbow / Willow Park). External geocoding stays REJECTED (Method 42.5); the unresolved rows await G-NAF property points or a Property_Key cadastre join.
- 43.3 Gazetteer additions (v44): Flagstone Water Play, Beenleigh Water Play, Forest Glen Water Feature - the water-park sites as PRINTED on the Pool Shop line items, absent from all three park datasets under those names. Underwood Water Feature -> Underwood Park; Logan Gardens Water Play -> Logan Gardens. Requeue: confirm the parent park of each water-play site with Parks (Flagstone Water Play sits in which named park?).
- 44.0 Batch 40 capture (v45). 46 corpus records, 44 primary and 2 duplicate copies. All 44 self-tie to the printed subtotal; every priced record is sourced from a TEXT line (none OCR-only); 64 of 64 pages represented; 121 of 121 priced records carry a numeric line amount. Rule 12 screen prefix-aware over all 28 EvID prefixes on Evidence_Invoices plus the bare id: 0 re-sightings. Corpus-internal normalised-text screen (v40 rule): 0 collisions. Every invoice matched to exactly ONE non-zero register row at the printed subtotal within 2c (amount corroboration, v35 rule); 28 $0.00 companion rows carry a cross-reference and no green block.
- 44.1 Defect family F1 recurs, third form (v45). `printed_total_incl_gst` held the SUBTOTAL on all 31 BrizSouth Locksmiths invoices (the printed TOTAL prints below the 'PLUS 10% GST' line and the same-line regex returned the subtotal). Restated as subtotal + printed GST and re-tied arithmetically on all 31; the restatement is stated in each anomalies field. Queue with the v33/v34/v36 findings for extraction prompt v3.
- 44.2 Site is printed on the invoice face for both vendors (v45). Q Power prints 'Site:' and 'Site Address:' as header rows; BrizSouth prints 'Job Location: <park>, <SUBURB>' in the description block. 41 of 44 invoices name a gazetteer park directly, so this batch is unusually rich for the site dimension: the printed site is written to Register DY with basis 'Sighted invoice site (printed)' on the sighted row and on its $0.00 companion. Two invoices print no site (C13293, C13403) and seven print no PK (15182, C13175, C13403, C13447, C13537, C13580, C13591) - Open Items #140.
- 44.3 Boilerplate (v45). Q Power prints one payment block and one terms notice, minted BP:QP-P1 / BP:QP-T1. BrizSouth reuses the existing BP:C-P1 (first sighted C13564) and gains BP:C-T1 for the 'WE APPRECIATE YOUR BUSINESS' notice. One key per cell (v34 trap).
- 45.0 Batch 41 identification (v46). Five APLEDGER creditor histories: ELE032 Electrical Data & Security Services Pty Ltd (4,444 invoices, ABN 17 099 805 277), QPO001 Q Power (Qld) Pty Ltd (1,131, ABN 82 067 507 591), SEC022 (24, ABN 97 613 325 769), SEA029 Sea-Crete (1, ABN 84 127 054 291), HIB003 (2, ABN 30 306 518 188). 485 register rows identified, $330,915.43. Amount corroboration is the gate (v35): 20 references matched on reference but failed the incl/1.1 test within 2c and are HELD. 67 Confirmed rows skipped. Closes four of the six unidentified series: FY2023/24 7-digit and FY2024/25 7-digit (ELE032, shared with SEC022 - the 7-digit series is multi-vendor), FY2024/25 and FY2025/26 5-digit (QPO001, resolving Open Item #89 - the amount test separates Q Power from Logan Plumbing cleanly).
- 45.1 A published address is a starting point, not an authority (v46, standing rule). The Parks Directory lists Ewing Park at Netball Drive and Rainbow Park at Ewing Road, so an address match sent all 237 lines at 99 Ewing Rd Woodridge to Rainbow Park. The user corrected it to Ewing Park. Street-ONLY matching is now banned outright: it sent '293 Logan St Eagleby' to Doug Larsen Park, which is 41 Logan Street BEENLEIGH, a cross-suburb error on 42 lines. Only street PLUS suburb may be proposed, and only as a flagged proposal.
- 45.2 NEW sheet Site_Crosswalk (v46). Manual, user-adjudicated site decisions with the basis recorded. The crosswalk OUTRANKS the automatic matcher and is applied on every build, so local knowledge survives a rebuild. Seeded with seven decisions including the removal of 'Shailer Park', which is a SUBURB and David Robinson Landscaping's business address (15 Helios St, Shailer Park, per PO 802122): it had taken $50,140 across 16 lines and beat 'Homestead Park (Shailer Park)' on an exact match.
- 45.3 Non-park Council facilities are sites (v46). Olivers Sports Complex, West Logan Netball Courts, the Historical Society Amenities and the Logan and District Services Club are real Council sites carrying real spend; they are recorded under the basis 'Non-park Council facility (named site, not a park)' rather than forced onto a park or left unresolved. This extends the Parks Depot precedent (v43) from one facility to a class.
- 46.0 Customer Request register join (v47). Parks Services V2.1 `_AGR_Data`, 12,190 customer requests carrying Location, Suburb, Division, Task (PK-bearing), Contractor, Cost Estimate, Actual Cost and Invoice Number. Joined to the Register on the invoice number, normalised by stripping every non-alphanumeric character ('INV 2428' and 'INV-2428' are one key). 1290 previously unattributed register lines gain a site under the basis 'Customer Request register (Location, invoice-number join)' (Tier 2 - an operational tracker, not an accounting record).
- 46.1 THE INVOICE IS AUTHORITATIVE (standing rule, v47). A row already allocated per printed invoice line (basis 'Multiple sites named (allocated per printed line, see Site_Allocation)') is NEVER overwritten by the CR join - {n_ma_skip} rows protected at v47; overwriting one double counts its dollars (register column T in Panel A plus its own Site_Allocation rows) and breaks both the Sites tie and the Site_Allocation tie. Where a register line already carries a site derived from invoice evidence and the CR Location disagrees, the register site STANDS and the CR reading is appended to column {BC} as '[CR DISAGREES: ...]' and written to Site_Crosswalk. {n_flag} such lines at v47. The CR register is an operational tracker: its Actual Cost disagrees with the register on roughly half the matched lines, so it is used for the SITE only, never for the amount, the PK or the vendor.
- 46.2 CR register data-quality gates (v47). Location values 'Various', '0', blank and 'Scheduled' are junk and excluded; 52 invoice keys carry more than one distinct Location and are skipped rather than allocated (the multi-site principle); one Location field holds a product description ('Abus 83/45 Titanium Padlocks...'), so free-text Location is resolved against the gazetteer and never taken verbatim.
- 46.3 Gazetteer harvested from the CR `Lists` sheet (v47). The Parks Services workbook maintains a curated Park_Name list, 275 names, of which 146 were absent from the register gazetteer. This is a Council-maintained operational list and resolves most of the outstanding candidate names without individual adjudication. Names appearing in the CR list but never in the register carry no spend and simply widen the match surface.
- 46.4 F82 Landscaping (v47). The CR register names the contractor 'F82 Landscaping'; the AP ledger carries 'The Trustee for Flavell-Dau Family Trust' (THE289, ABN 47 220 358 629). Recorded as a trading name on Vendor_Series so both spellings resolve.
- 47.0 Collect-all-candidates (v48, standing rule). The site read no longer stops at the first non-empty field. Every register column outside the ban list (12, 25, 32, 92, 98, 105) is read, every candidate collected, and the decision made afterwards on the priority order. `(not printed)` and `(blank as printed)` are EMPTY, never an answer: at v47 they stopped the scan on 80 sighted rows carrying $340,399 whose site sat in another field. Where candidates agree the highest-priority source is written; where they name several parks the row becomes multi-site.
- 47.1 Suburb blocklist as a first-class object (v48). All 70 Logan suburbs are loaded and the build ASSERTS that no gazetteer label is a suburb and that no suburb is ever written as a site. Banning column 92 at v43 was not enough - Shailer Park entered through other fields and took $50,140. The blocklist is the structural fix.
- 47.2 Joined multi-site lists are re-screened on every build (v48). 193 lists reduced to a single park once blocked terms were removed - `Haldham Park; Shailer Park`, `Lansdown Park; Shailer Park`, `Kimberley Forest Park; Shailer Park`, `Larry Storey Park; Shailer Park`, `Shailer Park; Shailer Pioneer Park`. A gazetteer change must always trigger a list re-screen; the v46 removal fixed single-site rows only.
- 47.3 Evidence_Invoice_Lines site written across the WHOLE sheet (v48). At v47 only the Batch 40 lines carried a line-level site, so 584 of 594 EIL lines tied to unresolved register rows were blank. Now every one of the 4956 data rows carries a site and a basis; 1106 name a park.
- 47.4 Marsden Stores catalogue (NEW v48). The internal stores issues narrate `Despatch Stock Requisition 'nnnnnn'-ALLSTORE` and carry a six or seven digit PRODUCT number in the GL narration and Src Details. Matched against the Marsden Stores 2026 Inventory Catalogue (293 product numbers parsed from the PDF), 109 of the 326 internal stores rows resolve to a named item - paver sand 20kg, galvanised post 50mm x 4.4m, ground anchor spikes, star pickets, 1-ply toilet paper, first aid kits. This identifies WHAT was issued; it does NOT give a site, because a stores issue records that goods left the store, not where they were installed. Recorded in the Coding Note.
- 47.5 Tolls are not site-specific (v48). Toll operator lines are reclassified from `No site determinable` to `Not site-specific by nature (tolls, network charges)`. A toll has no park by nature; leaving it in a residual class overstates the unresolved position.
- 48.0 User web-verified address decisions (v49). The 578 address-to-park questions were adjudicated by the user against public sources (Council directory, LPS2015 ArcGIS, property records) with a status per row: VERIFIED (5, official Council source), SUPPORTED (104, unique directory match), PROBABLE (1), AMBIGUOUS (28, more than one park on the street), NO UNIQUE PUBLIC MATCH (439), NOT A PARK (1: 90-110 Evans Road Meadowbrook, a Council-rated parcel - note Open Item #123 sits on the same parcel). Every decision is written to Site_Crosswalk with its status and source URL, so it outranks the matcher permanently and unresolved rows are never re-asked. Only rows with no site from evidence were written; allocated-multi rows untouched.
- 48.1 Local knowledge outranks a public listing (v50, standing rule). The web-verification pass marked 90-110 Evans Road Meadowbrook NOT A PARK on a property-portal search; the user's local knowledge is that it IS Riverdale Park. The Council-rated parcel (S07 9714148, Open Item #123) is the park's own parcel. Corrected on Site_Crosswalk with the override recorded and the superseded status retained. Rule: a public property listing describes tenure, not use; it can neither confirm nor deny that a Council parcel is a park.
- 49.0 Batch 42 capture (v51). Corpus qpower_historical_20260818 (Q Power.pdf, 118 pages, 51 primary documents, 0 duplicate copies; extraction Microsoft 365 Copilot, GPT-5 reasoning model). Every invoice's printed subtotal, GST and total re-tied (GST = ROUND_HALF_UP(subtotal/10), subtotal + GST = total, header AMOUNT = total); rule 12 screen prefix-aware over all 28 EvID prefixes plus the bare id: 0 re-sightings; corpus-internal normalised-text screen: 0 collisions. 48 invoices matched to exactly ONE non-zero AP row at the printed subtotal within 2c (amount corroboration, v35 rule) with the register Doc Date agreeing to the day; no $0.00 companions. Three invoices carry no register line and are captured under rule 16 only (v36 unmatched pathway).
- 49.1 The Q Power work-note template prints NO line table (v51, extends the v40 BLC single-scope precedent). 32 of 51 invoices (Aug-2024 to Jun-2025) print a Description / Work Note narrative and a totals block only - no priced item grid on the text layer OR the OCR layer (the extractor's 'OUT' set). Each is captured as ONE single-scope line = the printed Description block at the printed Sub-Total ex GST, qty / unit '(not printed)', with the basis stated in the EIL Notes and the anomalies field; check 1 ties by construction and checks 2 and 3 tie on the printed figures. This is rule 16 line capture at the granularity the invoice prints, not a summary row: the invoice has one scope. If Finance requires a labour / materials breakdown for these 32 invoices it must be requested from Q Power (Open Items #160).
- 49.2 F1, fourth form (v51): the extractor's invoice_date is the WORK NOTE date or the PLEASE PAY BY date. The Q Power header row prints 'PLEASE PAY BY | AMOUNT | INVOICE DATE' as three values on one row ('Invoicing 05/09/2024 $712.80 22/08/2024'); the corpus invoice_date disagreed with the printed INVOICE DATE on 36 of 51 documents (work-note date) and, in the Batch 40 corpus, on all 13 v45 Q Power captures (the pay-by date). Restated from the header row - due date first, invoice date third - and cross-checked against the register Doc Date on every matched row (48 of 48 agree). The 13 v45 green blocks (15133-15198) are repaired in this build from the retained Batch 40 page text: Invoice Date and Due Date rewritten on the register and on Evidence_Invoices, the repair stated in the anomalies. Seven v28 captures (QP-15124/15137/15138/15147/15158/15159/15169) show the same 14-day pattern but their page text was not retained; flagged, not rewritten (Open Items #159).
- 49.3 F12 on the Q Power simPRO line table (v51). Wrapped items print over THREE layout rows - description above, part number / item on the priced row, continuation below ('DIN Contactor 1P 8.5A AC3 25A AC1 230V | HAGESC125 | 1NO DIN Rail'). Rule: a NARRATIVE row immediately above a priced row is its prefix; when a prefix exists the NARRATIVE row immediately below is its suffix; every narrative row inside the table block must be consumed (asserted) and every row asserted verbatim in the retained page text. Site / Site Address / Order No. print as label-led rows with continuation rows below; the description block runs from 'Description' to 'Ordered By:'; work notes run from the first '(dd/mm/yyyy) - Work Note' header to the table.
- 49.4 Batch 43: Origin collective account summaries A-FE35E476-022 to -027 (v51). The six 'Your collective account summary' PDFs (Sep-2025 to Feb-2026 issues) print the CURRENT CHARGES - SUB ACCOUNT SUMMARY: sub account, invoice number, full supply address, GST and charges incl GST for every Council sub-account (233 / 301 / 396 / 202 / 199 / 298 accounts). Parsed with the account count and the printed totals asserted per statement. Every register (statement, NMI) group - 375 groups, 1191 lines, $42,265.95 ex GST - was matched to a statement sub-account by supply-address prefix (the register supply string is cut at 40 characters, so a truncated tail token is dropped before comparison) and ties register ex GST to (charges incl GST - GST) to the cent: 375 of 375. With v42 (-021, -028 to -031) the FY2025/26 collective account is now reconciled on eleven statements; -020 (14 lines) and -033 (148 lines) remain. The full supply addresses add no park name the truncated register labels did not already carry, so no site write follows from Batch 43. Worked in PSWP_Batch43_Electricity_Reconciliation_022_027.xlsx.
- 49.5 Site decisions applied (v51). Thirty user-adjudicated candidate names (Add/Merge) folded into the alias map and Site_Crosswalk: spelling variants (Jamie Nicholson -> Jamie Nicolson Park, Doug Larson -> Doug Larsen Park, Teviot Down / Tervit Down -> Teviot Downs Park, Hubener -> Hubner Park, Skinner -> Skinners Park, Lavell -> Lavelle Park, Richared Wilson -> Richard Wilson Park, Woodland District -> Woodlands District Park, Forest Glen -> Forestglen Park, Shaw St -> Shaw Street Oval, Oliver / Oliver's Sport(s) / Vandalism Olivers -> Olivers Sports Complex, Colville St Green -> Colville Park, Clean Up Cecil Clark -> Cecil Clark Park, Alexandra -> Alexander Park, Darlington Park -> Darlington Parklands, Logan Gardens Park -> Logan Gardens, Springwood Dog Off Leash Park -> Springwood Park, Harley Street -> Harley St Shailer Park, Mt Warren Oval Park added as printed (the target Mount Warren Park is a suburb name, blocklisted), Logan Reserve -> Logan Reserve Park [guarded], Jamie Nicholson Park Lavelle Park -> two parks); five confirmed as existing labels. Applied three ways: (a) alias tokens sitting in DY itself canonicalised, joined lists re-screened; (b) rows whose deciding source column (priority 111 > 106 > 112 > 108 > 127 > 99 > 42 > 22 > 23 > 80) names an alias re-decided; a manual, CR-register, rates-parcel or user-adjudicated basis is never overwritten by the matcher. 92 DY/DZ writes. The v48 variant map's 'Logan Gardens Park -> Logan Garden Park' (a non-existent label) is corrected. 17 Peacock Avenue = Logan River Parklands: the Origin label 'Logan River Park' (46 lines, all at Lot 2 Peacock Ave) merged into Logan River Parklands. 71 Cinderella Drive = Springwood Park was already applied (v49); recorded.
- 50.0 Batch 44 capture (v52). Corpus levai_19_08 (Levai 19.08.pdf, 58 pages, 58 document records = 49 unique invoices + 9 duplicate copies; extraction M365 Copilot, GPT-5 reasoning model). Every invoice re-tied (captured lines = printed subtotal, GST = ROUND_HALF_UP(subtotal/10), subtotal + GST = total); within-corpus duplicate screen by normalised text confirms the extractor's 9 duplicate copies; rule 12 screen prefix-aware over 28 EvID prefixes plus the bare id against 199 existing Levai captures: 0 re-sightings. 44 invoices matched to exactly ONE non-zero AP row at the printed subtotal (amount corroboration, v35 rule) with the register Doc Date agreeing to the day; no companions, no splits, no check variants. Five FY2019/20 invoices predate the loaded scope and are captured under rule 16 only (v36 unmatched pathway).
- 50.1 Two Levai templates in one binder (v52). The Xero template (INV-#, from Mar-2020) prints officer / contract / PK / 'CR - Site (Ref: n)' / 'Completed - date' above a DESCRIPTION-AMOUNT table with descriptions wrapping onto rows above and below the priced row (F12; every row asserted verbatim and joined ' | '), GST (10%) on the totals block only. The LEGACY template (8-digit number, letterhead 34-36 Calcium Court CRESTMEAD, to Dec-2019) prints Contact / Job# / Contract head rows, a 'JOB NAME - <CR> - <site> (Ref: n)' job line, '$' amounts, 'Terms: Net 14' and no due date; 00022061 prints one line per park (15 parks at $185.00 under a 'DIVISION 6' group row). Three legacy invoices are IMAGE-ONLY pages read from the OCR layer with the figures cross-checked arithmetically; visual transcription against the source PDF is outstanding (v39 route, Open Items #167).
- 50.2 REFERENCE COLLISION TRAP, third form (v52): Levai's legacy 8-digit numbers strip to the register's FY2023/24 5-digit Levai references (00020982 -> 20982 row 885, 00021470 -> 21470 row 389, 00021477 -> 21477 row 855, 00022061 -> 22061 row 401). All four hit a register row on the bare number, four years apart, and all four fail amount corroboration; row 885 even carries 'Hubner Park' from its enquiry narration, matching the 2019 invoice's site. Amount corroboration (v35) is what separates them; the four are held under rule 16 with no register line.
- 50.3 New corpus defect (v52): OCR fragment classified PRICED. On an image-only page the printed 'Paid to Date: $0.00' shreds to a $0.00 PRICED record (00020982); it does not disturb the self-tie but would write a phantom EIL line. Zero-amount PRICED records on OCR pages are dropped with the basis in the anomalies. Also: a dd.mm.yyyy 'Completed - date' row matches a naive amount regex (28.08) - an amount-row scan must exclude date tokens or every Levai invoice reads as an F9 loss (false positive caught this session).
- 50.4 Site dimension (v52). Every Levai invoice names its site on its own face. 37 matched rows gain 'Sighted invoice site (printed)' (CARVERS PARK and Noffke Park Bethania resolved to the gazetteer labels; P0K000022 normalised to PK000022 with the printed form retained). 7 printed sites are not gazetteer labels and are HELD in the candidate-harvest class (Anzac Park (Kingston), Buttuta Park, Heritage Boulevard Park, Jacaranda Avenue, James Smith Recreation Area, Larry Storey Boat Ramp, Latimer Road Reserve) - Open Items #168. Thirty-two v51 worklist decisions applied through Site_Crosswalk: 12 candidate names resolved to Council parks-directory entries (7 new gazetteer labels: Heather Park, River Hills Park West, Duchess Park, Leighton Fields, Mundoolun Park, Sunridge Park, Schmidts Creek Park North; Princess Park Marsden = J J Smith Park; Jimboomba Sports Park = Jimboomba Park; Tygum Skate Park = Tygum Park; Mount Warren Boulevard = Mt Warren Oval Park; Leighton Fields Park = Leighton Fields; Mundoolum = Mundoolun Park), 22 rows (177 Chambers Flat Road x9, weekly skate-park inspections x13) moved to No site determinable on the user's decision. Panel A rebuilt: 558 sites.
- 51.0 Batch 45 capture (v53). Corpus david_robinson_tax_invoice (David Robinson TAX INVOICE.pdf, 82 pages, 82 documents, 0 duplicate copies; extraction M365 Copilot, GPT-5 reasoning model). Every invoice re-tied (captured lines = printed subtotal, GST = ROUND_HALF_UP(subtotal/10), subtotal + GST = total); within-corpus normalised-text screen 0 collisions; rule 12 screen prefix-aware over 38 EvID prefixes plus the bare id: ONE re-sighting (11546 = DRL-11546, v36 Batch 33), audited against the existing Evidence_Invoices row on subtotal, GST, total, line count and date - all agree - and not re-captured. 81 invoices matched to exactly ONE non-zero AP row at the printed subtotal (v35 amount corroboration) with the register Doc Date agreeing to the day; no companions, no splits, no check variants. Contract LB304 to Sep-2023, PAR/335J/2023 after.
- 51.1 Robinson template (v53, extends v36). Charges print per site under a 'SITE - CR n - OFFICER' header row; the description wraps over 1-5 rows and is joined with a space (F12; every row asserted verbatim); the header is prefixed to each charge under it. Three Jul-2023 invoices (10949, 10951, 10952) print an EARLY free-form layout: date, site, suburb, CR#### and officer inline in mixed case with no ' - CR n - ' separators, and once (10951) the header row itself carries the amount; parsed by the CR token. 10952 prints its date month/day/year ('7/28/2023'), read as 28-Jul-2023 and corroborated by the register Doc Date (F1, fifth form).
- 51.2 Site dimension read at the printed line (v53, v44 two-level model). Each printed line's park is the header site resolved to a gazetteer label (case-folded; trailing suburb tokens stripped; Site_Crosswalk park aliases applied - OLIVER SPORTS COMPLEX -> Olivers Sports Complex, LOGAN RIVER KIEWA DRIVE -> Kiewa Park). A register row whose lines all name ONE park reads that park (16 rows); a row whose lines name several parks, or one park plus unnamed lines, reads 'Multiple sites named (allocated per printed line, see Site_Allocation)' with its dollars exploded per printed line on Site_Allocation and a residue row for the unnamed lines (59 rows, +176 allocation rows); a row whose lines name no gazetteer park is held in the candidate-harvest class (6 rows). 33 printed header sites do not resolve and are flagged on Site_Crosswalk for adjudication (Open Items #175); several are evident typos of gazetteer labels (BEG PARK, DAILSY HILL PARK, HOMESTED PARK) but a typo is not corrected without the user's decision.
- 51.3 Findings (v53). 11423 prints Date 01/08/2023 for an invoice in the Aug-2024 sequence with July-2024 work dates; TechOne carries the printed 2023 date as the Doc Date and posts it FY2024/25 (Open Items #173). 11516 line 4 ($250.00) is printed as an attendance charge for a job already completed under CR 3592347 ('a double up'); 11354 flags a locking-rail request as a possible duplicate of CR 3530977 (Open Items #174). 1 printed-PK divergences (Open Items #172).
- 52.0 The v44 rule is retrospective, not per batch (v54). Each site build applied 'never hold at register level what the printed lines beneath it already allocate' to the rows it was writing, so rows captured before the two-level model, or captured in a batch that did not re-read them, stayed at 'Multiple sites named (not allocated)' even though Evidence_Invoice_Lines column L already named the park on every priced line. 32 such rows survived to v53 ($191,519.97). Exploded here from the printed line amounts with a residue row per LineKey; the split is read, never apportioned. STANDING RULE: every site build re-tests EVERY 'Multiple sites named (not allocated)' row against its tied printed lines, not only the rows in its own batch.
- 52.1 What genuinely stays unallocated (v54). 54 rows ($151,174.41) remain. Three causes, all evidential: (a) the printed lines price a SERVICE, not a site - Harpley HSTP rounds bill 'Site & Soil Designs 3 @ $1,350', 'Labour 3 @ $720' across four or eight named parks, so no printed amount attaches to a park (9 rows); (b) the invoice prints ONE lump sum and lists the sites in the narrative only - Q Power QP-14301 $6,241.80 over six parks, Shell Energy, T2 (9 rows); (c) the capture dropped the per-site header - 36 David Robinson rows from the v36 Batch 33 capture ($105,261.00), where the invoice DOES print 'SITE - CR n - OFFICER' above each charge (proved by the Batch 45 parse of the same template at v53) but the v36 build wrote neither the header into the description nor the site into column L. Only (c) is recoverable, and only by re-sighting those invoices (Open Items #177).
- 53.0 Batch 46 capture (v55). 71 invoices from the harpley_levai v3 corpus captured under rules 16 and 17: Harpley Services Pty Ltd 37 (template A, contract LCC-09-2022) and T & H Levai Pty Ltd 34 (Xero template, LB304 then PAR/335L/2023). 129 printed lines, $67,358.39 ex GST. All 71 register rows green-blocked Confirmed on the standard check forms - no check variants, so VARIANT_TAG_MIN and GSTINC_ALLOWANCE are unchanged. Every target was selected by printed subtotal and amount-corroborated to the cent; 00014430 also matched a Q Power row on the bare reference 14430 at $425.00 and was rejected on amount (Method 34.2 / 50.2, third confirmation of the reference-collision rule).
- 53.1 A clean v3 corpus is possible (v55). This is the first v3 extraction corpus in the project on which the F1 family does not fire: printed_subtotal, printed_gst and printed_total are correct on all 71 documents, invoice_date is correct on all 71, and the self-tie gate agrees with the in-session restatement to the cent. Both templates print their totals block on one layout row per figure, which is what the F1 findings of v33, v34, v36, v45 and v51 asked the extraction prompt to handle. The gates were run in full regardless; a green extractor report is never taken on trust (v40).
- 53.2 The text layer can truncate a header VALUE (new trap, v55). On the Harpley header band the text layer cuts the Purchase Order to its first digit ("Purchase Order: 7") because the value sits in a narrow right-hand column; the OCR layer of the same page prints it in full ("Purchase Order: 703350") and the corpus po_refs carries it. Where a field is present but truncated in the text layer, restate from the invoice own OCR layer and assert the token there - do not record the truncation as the printed value and do not treat the field as "(not printed)".
- 53.3 A per-site round is allocated from its own printed lines (v55, v44 model). Harpley 00013661 prints "PK#: n/a", "CR/WO#: n/a" and "Site Details: Various", so at register level it names no site - but its eight printed lines each name a park and carry their own amount. The row is therefore allocated on Site_Allocation across seven gazetteer parks ($700.00) with an $80.00 residue for the one printed name the gazetteer does not carry, and its DY holds the joined list of every printed site named, resolved or not, so Panel A cannot double count it. This is the v54 standing rule applied at capture rather than retrospectively.
- 53.4 Findings (v55). One sighted Harpley scope posts across three natural accounts and one sighted Levai scope breaks account at the FY2023/24 to FY2024/25 boundary (Open Items #178, #179); seven printed-versus-charged PK divergences (#180); 15 printed site names outside the gazetteer (#181); INV-36524 prints an internally inconsistent chronology (#182); the Harpley and Levai payment blocks are each held under five boilerplate keys differing only in separators (#183); Panel A carries two spellings of Shailer Pioneer Park (#184).
- 54.0 Batch 47 capture (v56). 56 Star Carpentry invoices from the star_carpentry v3 corpus captured under rules 16 and 17: 113 printed lines, $59,847.00 ex GST, one invoice per page. All 56 register rows green-blocked Confirmed on the standard check forms - no check variants, so VARIANT_TAG_MIN and GSTINC_ALLOWANCE are unchanged. Every target was selected by printed subtotal and amount-corroborated to the cent, which matters here because the INV-#### space is multi-vendor across this register.
- 54.1 Star Carpentry (Xero) template. A DESCRIPTION / AMOUNT table with no qty, no unit price and no per-line GST column; GST prints once on the totals block as "TOTAL GST 10%". The head of the description block prints a job line - "Job CR#nnnnnnn <site> <SUBURB> PKnnnnnn <officer>" - in nine observed arrangements: the PK and officer on the same row or the next row, the officer before or after the PK, "Job CR#", "Job CR", "Job #" or a bare "#" before the seven-digit number, and one invoice with no job number at all. Work-description rows follow, then the priced lines (typically a materials line and a labour line). The PAYMENT ADVICE remittance slip prints at the foot of the same page.
- 54.2 A site is derived from the head line only where the job number anchors it (v56). On INV-9470 the head line reads "Sign Cabinet 600m wide - PK000022 - option 2 -Jessica Burke": no job number, and the residue after removing the PK and the officer is a work description, not a place. Without the job-number anchor the head line is captured as the work description and the row takes "No site determinable". Stripping tokens out of a free-text line and calling the remainder a site is how a suburb or a supplier address gets in (v43, v46, v48).
- 54.3 Trailing work text after a park name (v56). Two invoices print the work in the same breath as the site - "Hyde Park Wire Balustrade", "Crestmead Park Table Repair". Where the leading words match a gazetteer label exactly and the trailing text runs to three words or fewer and carries no park-suffix word (Park, Reserve, Gardens, Parklands, Oval, Bushland, Green, Square, Common, Complex, Forest, Buffer), the leading label is taken and the trailing text is recorded in the anomalies. The rule is deliberately narrow: it decides that trailing text is NOT part of the name, never which place a name means (v55).
- 54.4 Findings (v56). A third creditor shows the FY-boundary account break, 73212 in FY2023/24 and 73123 from FY2024/25 on identical scope (Open Item #185, after Higgins #105 and Levai #179); no contract reference is printed on any of the 56 invoices, only a purchase-order number in the Xero Reference field (#186); six printed-versus-charged PK divergences (#187); INV-8819 prints a due date seventeen days BEFORE its invoice date (#188); INV-9663 prints no PK and INV-9470 prints no job number and names no site (#189); ten printed park names sit outside the gazetteer, all ten corroborated by the Customer Request register and one (Buttuta Park) by the Council-maintained Lists column (#190).
- 55.0 The Council park register is the authoritative gazetteer (v57). The "List of parks" export - 988 named parks, each with an asset number and a street address - supersedes every earlier gazetteer source: the S07 rates-narration harvest (v42), the public Parks Directory (G4), and the 275-name Parks Services Lists column (v47). It was supplied in place of the two returned site worklists, and it answers them: of the 25 names those worklists held, 17 are in the register and resolve, and 8 are not park names at all.
- 55.1 A label is added only where it claims a register row (v57). The register carries 988 names against a Panel A of 558; adding all 527 absent names would fill the cost-per-park panel with parks that carry no Parks Services spend and make it useless as a management view. Panel A is a COST panel, not a park list. The full register is held as the match surface for every future build; only the 42 names that actually claim a held row were added as Panel A rows.
- 55.2 A hold pending evidence is answered, not reversed (v57, refines the v55 rule). Site_Crosswalk carries two different things in column B: a positive DECISION AGAINST, written "(removed)" - the Shailer Park suburb ban - and an UNDECIDED question, written "HELD - candidate harvest". The v55 rule that a crosswalk decision is not overturned by later corroboration applies to the first. The second is a question, and authoritative Council evidence answers it: 26 holds were released at v57 and every one is recorded with the register as its basis. "(removed)" entries were not touched.
- 55.3 Three guards the widened scan needed (v57). (a) The ship-to column was dropped from the re-screen: the Harpley and Star Carpentry letterheads print "Logan City Council Parks Depot, 177 Chambers Flat Road" on EVERY invoice, so scanning it attributed six held rows to the depot regardless of where the work was (v43: Council own address is never site evidence). (b) A label immediately followed by a park-suffix word is a PREFIX of a longer name the gazetteer does not carry, not a match - "Village Green" inside "Village Green Park" (the v51 substring trap). (c) Where a label that is also a suburb appears alongside another label in the same field it is the suburb - "Hyde Park, REGENTS PARK" names one park, not two.
- 55.4 What the wider gazetteer did NOT fix (v57). 297 candidate-harvest rows still name no park in any readable field; their source text is scope wording, not a place. 1,138 address-only rows still need a property-level geocode - although the Council register now supplies a street address for all 988 parks, which is the index the v43 address route was rejected for lacking (Open Items #131, #194).
- 56.0 A decision appended after the scan is not applied (build trap, v57, repaired v58). The v57 build wrote its Site_Crosswalk additions in section 5, AFTER the resolution scan in section 4 had already decided every row. The one decision minted in that section - James Smith Rec Reserve -> James Smith Recreation Area - was therefore recorded on the crosswalk and never applied, leaving register row 2750 in the candidate-harvest class and an $80.00 printed line in the Site_Allocation residue. STANDING RULE: any build that mints a crosswalk decision re-runs the resolution over the FINAL crosswalk before it saves, and the verify re-tests every held row against the crosswalk as shipped. This is the v46 rule "manual decisions outrank the matcher" applied to decisions the build makes about itself.
- 57.0 A near-string match is not a duplicate (v59, correcting v57). The v57 build flagged six Panel A labels as probable mis-keys of a Council register name on a difflib ratio alone. Checked against the register properly - which carries a street and a suburb for every park - four of the six are eight DIFFERENT parks: Baroona (Brompton St, Rochedale South) and Barcoo (Barcoo Dr, Logan Reserve); Carvers (Carvers Way, Park Ridge) and Carter (Bannockburn Rd, Bannockburn); Carinya (Carinya St, Crestmead) and Cerina (Cerina Cct, Jimboomba); Presley (Ernest St, Windaroo) and Wesley (Wesley St, Marsden). STANDING RULE: a label pair is a duplicate only where one side is ABSENT from the Council register and the other is present. Both real typos passed that test - Buttata and Shailer Poineer are in no Council source.
- 57.1 The address route fails on the numbers, not the names (v59, closes the question #131 left open). The Council register supplies a street address for all 988 parks - exactly the index v43 wanted - and it still does not work: only 212 of the 988 addresses carry a house number, and the register column 42 supply string is truncated at 40 characters and usually drops the number before the street is reached. Zero number-and-street matches on 991 tested rows; 694 matched a street serving two or more parks. Nothing was written. The route needs G-NAF property points or a Property_Key join, and no better name list will substitute (Open Items #196).
- 57.2 Housekeeping cleared (v59). Open Item #95: 192 AP lines carried Status Confirmed on Nature Basis "Line narration" with no green block, set by an early creditor cross-match before rule 17 existed; they are now Partial with a sight-the-attachment follow-up. Open Item #94: 209 green-blocked rows still carried the pre-capture coding note and 333 the pre-capture follow-up, both contradicted by the green block on the same row; cleared. Neither pass touches identity, coding, amount or any control - the sighted-line controls never counted the #95 rows, which is why they survived so long.
- 58.0 The Parks Directory as a user decision instrument (v60). The user returned the v54 worklist with a Logan Parks Directory URL in place of a word: the link IS the decision and its evidence in one. Two of the five were confirmations of sites the Council register had already fixed at v57, which is the useful case - independent corroboration of an automated write. One, Jimmy Phillips Park -> Hillcrest Park, CONTRADICTS the Council asset register, which lists both as separate parks; the directory publishes them as one page. The user decision stands (v50: local knowledge outranks a Council operational record) and the conflict is recorded rather than buried (Open Items #202). A directory URL is recorded verbatim in the Site_Crosswalk basis so the decision can be re-checked against the source.
- 59.0 Batch 48 capture (v61). 35 Weis Contractors invoices from the weis_20260821 v3 corpus: 346 printed line-table rows, 157 of them priced, $59,053.45 ex GST. All 35 green-blocked Confirmed on the standard check forms - no check variants. Zero printed-versus-charged PK divergences, and the register Doc Date agrees with the printed invoice date on all 35.
- 59.1 A prefix collision is not a re-sighting, second confirmation (v61, the v34 rule). The rule 12 EvID screen returned HT-2098 and HT-2128 against corpus ids INV-2098 and INV-2128. They are Hilltops (Qld) Pty Ltd invoices at $299.00 each, not these Weis invoices at $2,105.00 each. The screen must be prefix-aware AND vendor-and-amount aware: a hit on the bare number across a different prefix family is a collision until the vendor and the amount agree.
- 59.2 The Weis line table carries the site dimension on its face (v61, v44 two-level model). Each priced work line sits under a ZERO-amount site heading row, with a zero-amount works-date row above the block. All 346 rows are captured under rule 16; each priced line takes the site heading printed immediately above it into Evidence_Invoice_Lines column L. A single-site invoice writes the park at register level; a multi-site invoice (22 of 35) reads "Multiple sites named (allocated per printed line)" and explodes on Site_Allocation at the printed line amounts, with a residue row for the printed names the gazetteer does not carry.
- 59.3 The printed suburb disambiguates two same-named parks (standing rule, v61). Panel A carries pairs that differ only by a parenthetical suburb - Rotary Park and Rotary Park (Jimboomba); Homestead Park and Homestead Park (Shailer Park); also Rotary Park (Beenleigh), Homestead Park (Jimboomba), Emerald Park (Regents Park) and Anzac Park (Kingston). Where the printed site text carries a suburb AND a "<name> (<Suburb>)" label exists, that label wins over the bare one. Stripping the suburb first and matching the bare label would have sent five Weis lines to the wrong Rotary Park.
- 59.4 The Reference value de-interleaves over up to three rows (v61, extends the v32 method). The Xero letterhead prints the Reference label and its value on separate layout rows interleaved with the bill-to and supplier-email columns, so the value arrives in two or three fragments ("CR - Rene" / "email/Alexander Clark" / "Park"). Fragments are collected at the column offset of the CR token, each is asserted verbatim in the page text, and the JOIN is stated in the anomalies as a reconstruction - the rule 19.2 gate rejected the join itself, which is what caught the two-row scan dropping a middle fragment.
- 59.5 Findings (v61). A fourth creditor shows the FY-boundary account break (#204, after #105, #179 and #185); the contract changes mid-series from PAR/327A/2020 to PAR/340A/2026 (#205); two printed site names sit outside the gazetteer (#206); INV-2224 prints a four-decimal unit price and rounds at invoice level (#207).
- 60.0 Batch 49 identification (v62). Seven APLEDGER creditor histories, 1,961 rows, none previously on Creditor_Lines. 19 register lines identified on amount corroboration at 1.1 and nothing on the reference alone; ten further reference matches were skipped because the register row is already Confirmed. Written through the identification write set only - creditor code, contractor, ABN, enquiry block, evidence, tier and basis. No green block was touched, no Status moved to Confirmed and column T was not written.
- 60.1 An ABN without a name is Tier 2, not Tier 1 (v62). Three of the seven accounts carry an ABN in the export and no trading name, and no invoice for them arrived in the bundle. They are recorded as "Unidentified (APLEDGER, ABN nn nnn nnn nnn)" at Tier 2 with a follow-up, not promoted to Tier 1 on the ABN alone: rule 8 requires the creditor history AND an identified vendor. The bank account name printed on one supporting invoice ("Shannon Tower Pty Ltd" on C00204561) was NOT taken as the trading name - a bank account name is not a letterhead (Open Items #209).
- 60.2 A supporting invoice in the same bundle names the account (v62). Four of the seven accounts were named from documents shipped alongside the histories rather than from the export: Elemental Shade Structures and T2 Electrical & Data were already on Vendor_Series and the invoices confirm the same ABN; 4Park Pty Ltd and Legacy Monuments are named on their own letterheads (C00224006, C00234587). Where a creditor-history export carries no name, look in the same delivery before recording the account as unnamed.
- 61.0 Batch 50 capture (v63). 60 Electrical Data & Security Services invoices from the edss_20260821 v3 corpus: 310 printed lines, $66,251.10 ex GST. All 60 green-blocked Confirmed on the standard check forms - no check variants. Every one of the 60 register rows was Pending evidence before this build, so the batch converts a whole Pending block to Confirmed in one pass.
- 61.1 EDSS (job-sheet) template (v63). The invoice prints "Tax Invoice : nnnnnnn" with the ABN in the header band, a long-form date ("Friday, 21 July, 2023"), a right-hand block of JOB NO / A/C CODE / ORDER NO, then a single job title row of the form "<Suburb>, <Park> - <Work>", then dated works-carried-out narrative blocks, then a DESCRIPTION / QTY / PRICE / AMOUNT table of dated labour hours (electrician and apprentice at separate rates), plant hire and materials. Per-line GST is not printed. The totals block prints GST Exclusive Total, GST and GST Inclusive Total on the last page.
- 61.2 Assert the PRINTED form of a date, not the normalised one (build trap, v63). The corpus carried invoice_date null on all 60 documents, and the restatement produced "21 Jul 2023" - a string that appears nowhere on the invoice, because the face prints "Friday, 21 July, 2023". The rule 19.2 gate rejected all 60. The printed long-form date is what is asserted and captured; the normalised date is derived from it for the date-typed fields. A restatement must be asserted in the form the document prints.
- 61.3 The job title row carries the site for the whole invoice (v63). Unlike the Weis per-line site model (v61), an EDSS invoice is one job at one site, so the title row names the site once and every printed line belongs to it. The site is taken from the segment of the title that resolves to a gazetteer label, with the leading suburb segment discarded by the suburb blocklist - note that the suburb prints FIRST on this template ("Jimboomba, Jimboomba Park"), the reverse of every other vendor in the register, and that "Mount Warren Park" is a suburb name that would otherwise have taken the site on 0055115 (the true site is Noyer Park). 56 of 60 resolve; nothing is allocated because nothing is split.
- 61.4 Findings (v63). All 60 invoices charge PK000412 across 56 different parks, with no PK printed on any face (#211, #213); the Nature Category was restated from the pre-sighting inference to Electrical & data services on all 60 (#212); four printed site names sit outside the gazetteer (#214).
- 62.0 Batch 51 capture (v64). Four creditor-attachment invoices, three vendors: T2 Electrical & Data 3219885 ($150.75, Evergreen Park RCD testing), Bunnings trade account 2768 8066/01231852 ($198.93) and 8066/01268248 ($49.58), Clean Management Solutions INV-1676 ($1,575.00, the PK000417 fortnightly round). 21 printed lines; sighted 2,316 -> 2,320. All four rows previously Pending evidence; all four amount-corroborated against their own APLEDGER creditor histories uploaded in the same session.
- 62.1 Bunnings Trade template (v64). Per-line GST PAYABLE and AMOUNT INCL GST columns print (per-line GST is captured as printed dollars, unlike the rate-column templates); DISCOUNT flags and product codes go to the line Notes (v34). 8066/01231852: the register posts the TechOne incl/1.1 derivation (198.91 against printed 198.93) - check 2 on the ratified derivation-equality form; printed TOTAL GST is the per-line rounding basket, 2c under 10% - check 3 on the ratified 2c tolerance (the Bethania Bunnings-entity precedent); and one printed RATE is a rounding of the unrounded unit price (2 x 21.65 = 43.30 v printed 43.31) - the AMOUNT column is authoritative, gate tolerance stated.
- 62.2 CMS (Xero) per-site round template, and the collision (v64). A head block prints "PK000417 / Fortnightly High Profile Cleaning" unpriced; one priced line per park, qty = visits at $75.00; each description joins two layout rows (F12). The register reference INV-1676 collides with a Weis Contractors invoice of the same number (row 1973, $915.00) - Method 34.2, resolved by EvID override CMS-1676, printed-subtotal target selection and DOCUMENT-ID companion scoping: the two $0.00 companion rows (16217, 16218) share the sighted row's Document Unique ID, which distinguishes them from the Weis posting's own companion (16183). Companion linkage by document id is stronger than the bare exactly-one-non-zero-row test and is the form to prefer where a reference collides. Three CMS invoices (INV-1693/1694/1696) were already captured at v18 under bare printed EvIDs and the Tier 1 label has sat on Vendor_Series row 125 since v18; the EvID override applies only where the printed id is a live collision.
- 62.3 v63 col-28 vendor text repaired (v64, Open Items #215). All 60 v63 EDSS captures cited "Weis Contractors" in the register col-28 evidence text (driver-derivation literal; everything else correct). Repaired from the row's own green-block vendor; the verify now asserts the col-28 vendor text names the col-L contractor on every green-blocked row, permanently.
- 62.4 Findings (v64). The five sighted Bunnings invoices spread one goods class across three natural accounts (#216); the sighted CMS face names six parks, not the two #210 described from the v62 bundle (#217); T2 prints PK000412 = charged, and its FY2023/24 posting sits on 73212 Major Contracts, consistent with the branch-wide FY2023/24 pattern (#105/#179/#185/#204).
- 63.0 Batch 52 identification (v65). Three full APLEDGER creditor histories (T2E002, BUN007, CLE030) identify 350 non-zero register lines Tier 1 with zero amount-test failures, plus 6 zero-amount companions via corroborated siblings; 48 "Bunnings Group (trade account 2768, series-inferred)" lines upgrade to the Tier 1 label. The Bunnings trade-account series (8066/8210/8171 references and the 13-digit no-slash vintage) and the CMS INV-# lines resolve; T2E002 becomes the THIRD vendor on the 7-digit series after ELE032 and SEC022 (v46) - amount corroboration, never the reference, is the separator.
- 63.1 The 16-column APLEDGER layout (v65). A third export vintage after the 26-column (v17) and 14-column (v55) shapes: system names row 4, display names row 5, data row 6+; the account code is stated only in the parameter line, which the parser asserts. Same standing traps: Transaction Amounts INCL GST, trailing blank-reference balance row excluded, Funds Transfer Paymt excluded; NEW this vintage - "Generated trans" rows referenced "Ledger Del" are TechOne archive consolidations (e.g. "742 Trans Del on 02/07/19"), not creditor invoices, and are excluded with the basis stated.
- 63.2 A re-export can contradict its own earlier code (v65, Open Items #218). The 20 v62 T2 lines carry code T2ELEC; the full export states Account = T2E002 and re-lists all 20 references identically. Content-screened out of the append (v55), NOT relabelled - the v62 source is discarded (rule 15), so neither code can be proven the artefact from here; the discrepancy is held for TechOne confirmation rather than silently rewritten.
