- 63.3 Purchase-card rows in a creditor history (v65, Open Items #219). BUN007 carries 360 PCard Expense rows - the first creditor-side evidence for the v40 purchase-card identification gap. They are preserved on Creditor_Lines and matched to no register row: PCard register rows are Confirmed on the system record and an identification build never touches a Confirmed row (v35).
- 64.0 Batch 53 capture (v66). A mixed-supplier bundle: 17 extracted documents, 16 suppliers, one PDF. Eleven new captures - Animal Management INV-3351 ($2,065.51), Green Frog Systems 005465 ($3,327.19), HIBB Electrical 7422 ($2,490.25), G.James 101936281 ($980.00), Play Force INV-2263 ($178.90), Weis INV-2263 ($348.00), 4Park INV103433 ($2,597.00), Legacy Monuments 2428 ($2,050.00), Parklands INV-0677 ($2,992.80), Flavell-Dau INV-0202 ($2,259.50), Ayson Industries 1928 ($2,680.00). 32 printed lines; sighted 2,320 -> 2,329. Nine of eleven green-blocked; two have no register line at all.
- 64.1 A mixed bundle is mostly duplicates (v66, rule 12). Six of the seventeen extracted documents were already on the workbook, four of them captured the SAME DAY at v64 - the screen is what stops a same-session re-capture, not the md5 gate, because the bundle is a new file carrying old facts (the v55 md5-then-content rule applied to invoices). Kachel 7546 arrived for the THIRD time (v32, v36, now v66) and remains the v30 HELD capture with no EvID on rows 205/241. A seventh hit, 1928, was a collision rather than a re-sighting: same bare number, different vendor, different amount (the v34 / v61 rule). Every one of the six was audited against its existing Evidence_Invoices row on subtotal, GST, total and priced-line count before being set aside.
- 64.2 Legacy Monuments: an invoice that prints no ex-GST subtotal (v66). The QuickBooks template prints RATE and AMOUNT columns GST-INCLUSIVE and a tax line reading "INCLUDES GST TOTAL", with no ex-GST figure anywhere on the face. Captured on the GST-inclusive basis (check 1 inclB, the v39 Harpley template B precedent), the recon row tied to the printed TOTAL, and GSTINC_ALLOWANCE moved +$205.00. The Printed Sub Total ex GST field carries an arithmetic derivation from TWO FIGURES ON THE SAME PRINTED FACE (TOTAL less INCLUDES GST TOTAL), stated in the anomalies with its basis. That is not a fill from another source and is the form to use where a template omits the ex-GST subtotal; the three checks then run on the standard forms for checks 2 and 3.
- 64.3 Two defect repairs on templates not seen before (v66). HIBB Electrical: the amount-bearing layout row is the electrical-safety CERTIFICATION CLAUSE, not the description - the F9 footer-clause family (v41 TradeTools). Repaired to one single-scope line at the printed Sub-Total, the description taken from the printed Description block, both rows asserted verbatim. Flavell-Dau: the priced row carries only the column LABEL ("Job Description:") and the amount, with the description on the row below - an F11/F12 hybrid, repaired by joining the two asserted rows. Both are the same underlying failure: an extractor that reads the amount column and takes whatever text shares its layout row.
- 64.4 The $0.00 STANDING ORDER shell family (v66, Open Items #224). 2,484 register rows over 2,335 distinct references carry $0.00 against a STANDING ORDER narration. Each has its OWN Document Unique ID and its own date, so a shell is never a companion of the invoice whose reference it repeats. INV-2263 is the clean demonstration: two different vendors print that number four months apart, both print PK000023, and the only PS/WP rows are two $0.00 PK000022 shells whose Doc Dates match the two invoices one for one. Neither shell was identified - a $0.00 row cannot be amount-corroborated and the family collides (v35, v65) - and both invoices were captured under rule 16 only.
- 64.5 Findings (v66, Open Items #220-#228). Green Frog Systems Pty Ltd names one of the three unnamed APLEDGER accounts of #209 (#221); HIBB 7422 posts $0.01 more than it claims, on the same document (#222); INV-2263, two vendors, neither posting in scope (#223); the shell family (#224); an Ayson row that was categorised as an insurance claim and is a bin-door handle pattern (#225); the Legacy Monuments inclusive-only face and its LEGACY / LEG006 creditor-code split (#226); one dog waste bag dispenser bought from two suppliers at two prices on two accounts (#227); and seven of eleven faces printing no PK, which is what makes the printed-versus-charged test unavailable on a mixed bundle (#228).
- 65.0 Batch 54 identification (v67). Four APLEDGER creditor histories - LEG006, BRI100, ELE013, GJA001 - identify 12 non-zero register lines with zero amount-test failures, $10,780.10 ex GST. The yield is small because three of the four were already on Creditor_Lines: what the batch really delivers is the authoritative account codes for three cohorts loaded at v62 under placeholders, and the whole G.James history, which resolves three lines sitting on three different unidentified 9-digit series labels.
- 65.1 A re-export that RESOLVES a code rather than contradicting one (v67, Open Items #229). At v62 three histories arrived with no account code and the build minted LEGACY, ELE-SHADE and ABN69 in session. The 26-column re-export states LEG006, ELE013 and BRI100 in its own parameter line and re-lists every reference identically. Because the earlier value was a self-declared placeholder and the new one is stated by the source, the relabel is the missing fact arriving, not a conflict - the opposite of Open Item #218, where two parameter lines disagree and nothing may be rewritten. 524 Creditor_Lines rows and 10 register rows relabelled. STANDING RULE: a placeholder code is replaced by an authoritative one; two authoritative codes in conflict are held, never merged.
- 65.2 The creditor-export ABN column is a transaction attribute, not an account attribute (v67, Open Items #230). Across the eight uploads the per-row ABN differs from the account ABN on a handful of rows in three accounts (T2E002 four of 1,662, BUN007 six of 2,537, ELE013 one of 429), including Levai's ABN appearing inside both the T2 and the Bunnings accounts. Identification now takes the account MODAL ABN and never a single row. Rule 8 is unaffected: on a SIGHTED INVOICE the printed ABN still decides.
- 65.3 The 26-column layout is read by NAME (v67). Three export vintages are now in the workbook - 26-column (v17), 14-column (v55) and 16-column (v65) - and the column ORDER differs between them (the due date sits at index 11 here and index 13 in the 16-column shape). The parser maps ATDocumentReference1, ATDVatDate, ATDocumentDate1, DTCDescription, Narration, CurrencyAmount, ATDueDate and the rest by system name into the Creditor_Lines field order, per the v13 26SLACT precedent. Reading by position across vintages would silently write a discount date into the due date column.
- 66.0 The content screen earns its keep a third time (v68, 24-Aug-2026). The rule 12 duplicate gate is md5 THEN CONTENT (v55). Third occurrence, and the first where the re-export is IDENTICAL rather than a different vintage of the same facts: four accounts re-exported one day after Batch 54, four new file md5s, and not one new row. Method: compare the two exports on the SYSTEM-NAME column set, then row-for-row on every business field EXCLUDING __md5Row, which TechOne recomputes per export and which therefore makes a byte-level file comparison useless. Where the row multiset matches exactly, the upload carries no fact and no build follows; where it does not, the difference is the batch. The earlier two occurrences were a 14-column re-export of five accounts already captured (v55) and the 21-Aug 26-column re-export that resolved three placeholder account codes (v67, Open Item #229) - a re-export can carry a new FACT even when it carries no new ROW, which is why the screen compares content rather than simply rejecting on account code.
- 67.0 The returned v68 worklist (v69). 33 decisions of a possible 884, on two of the six sheets. Sheet A resolved nine of 27 held park names and sheet C confirmed all 24 street-unique electricity attributions; sheets B, E and F came back blank and sheet D was removed from the workbook altogether. The blanks and the removal were both read as NO DECISION (v58): a worklist that comes back empty means no decision was made, never that the blank is an answer, and deleting a sheet is not a way of answering it either.
- 67.1 The URL is the decision AND its evidence (v69, third occurrence after v54 and v60). All nine sheet-A decisions were entered as Logan Parks Directory links rather than words. The label is taken from the URL slug and the link is stored verbatim in the Site_Crosswalk basis, so every decision can be re-checked against the source that made it. Two of the nine correct a printed error rather than naming a synonym - DAILSY HILL PARK is a typo for Daisy Hill Park and BEG PARK for Bega Park - which is exactly the class of name a string matcher can never resolve on its own.
- 67.2 Naming a park moves money out of the residue (v69). Each held name sat on a printed line of an allocated multi-site invoice, and because no gazetteer label matched, its printed amount fell into that invoice's residue row. Resolving the name moves the amount from the residue to the park: $6,990.00 across twelve lines and ten parks (a thirteenth line, on a pre-scope Levai invoice with no register line, gains the label only), and the Sites tie holds untouched because the two movements are equal and opposite on the same invoice. The multi-site rows themselves were not re-split - an allocated-multi row stays allocated as printed; only the LABEL changed.
- 67.3 A more precise directory creates ambiguity in a coarser gazetteer (v69, Open Items #235). The directory distinguishes Noffke Park Bethania from Noffke Park Meadowbrook while Panel A held a bare "Noffke Park"; it names Samuel and Agnes Smith Park while Panel A held "Smith Park". Neither was merged. A near-string match is not a duplicate without confirming one side is absent from the Council register, and here BOTH sides are present, so the merge needs a decision rather than a resemblance. The same restraint applied to Summerview Park (#236): the decision was against the suburb-qualified string "SUMMERVIEW PARK, YARRABILBA" and was applied ONLY there, leaving three rows that print the bare name untouched.
- 68.0 Batch 55 capture (v70). One bundle, three suppliers, 57 invoices, $77,034.89 ex GST, and not a single exception: no re-sighting, no reference collision, no amount-test failure, no printed-versus-charged PK divergence, and every target row already carrying the right vendor label. It closes 38 of the 6,910 lines classed No invoice sighted, which is the largest site class in the register.
- 68.1 The F10 family now includes a WRONG figure, not only a missing one (v70, Open Items #238). All eighteen Robinson documents carried printed_gst equal to the printed subtotal. The corpus self-tie passed regardless, because self-tie compares captured lines against the subtotal and never looks at the GST field. The parse gate that caught it is the independent one: printed GST must equal ROUND_HALF_UP(subtotal / 10) AND subtotal plus GST must equal the printed total. A green extraction gate proves amounts, not fields.
- 68.2 Capture the structure even when you do not use it (v70, Open Items #239). Twenty-one invoices name several parks. They are held unallocated, because a multi-site row is never split on an assumption - but both templates print a per-site heading immediately above that site's priced lines, and those headings are captured verbatim on Evidence_Invoice_Lines (the Weis ones as $0.00 printed rows under rule 16). The allocation can therefore be made later from the workbook alone, with no re-sighting. That is the whole argument for capturing zero-amount printed rows.
- 68.3 Where the invoice is silent, it does not overrule (v70). The invoice outranks every later source on what it PRINTS; silence is not evidence. Of the thirteen faces naming no park, nine had no attribution to lose and move to No site determinable, while FOUR already carried a Customer Request register attribution and keep it - the sighted face neither confirms nor contradicts it. Overwriting those four would have destroyed evidence in the name of a higher tier.
- 56.0 Batch 56 capture (25-Aug-2026, v71). Four streams in one rule 16/17 build: the Robinson page-line corpus `david` (50 invoices, 110 priced lines, all at TIE; the corpus retains the full 80-column text layer, so EIL descriptions are the printed lines verbatim and each priced line note carries its printed site/CR heading per Open Items #239); Sea-Crete 7558 and TBM 00097583/00097626 by the raw-text route; and the rule 17 COMPLETION of the v30 HELD Kachel 7546 capture on the Document Reconstruction evidence (check-2 sum-tie variant, [check variant] tagged). Green blocks 55 rows; EI +53 (KC-7546 not re-captured, rule 12); recon 2,517 -> 2,570; sighted 2,386 -> 2,441; variant floor 882 -> 884. Instructions v10 adopted and embedded this build (consolidation of rules 1-19 plus rule 20; check-2 sum-tie and partial-scope folded into rule 17). Citation limbs rewritten from the EvID maps on the recon relocation (19.10).
- 57.0 Batch 57 identification (25-Aug-2026, v72). Three refreshed APLEDGER account tables in the 12-column Ledger Accounts Transactions Table vintage (a FOURTH export shape; read by system name per the v13 rule - this vintage carries NO per-row ABN, no period, no ageing). Amount corroboration at 1.1 within 2c is the gate on every one of the 285 written rows (v35); the reference alone identified nothing. A Confirmed row is never an identification target: the two v71-sighted TBM rows took only the placeholder code sweep (the b67 Legacy precedent). SEC022 restated Tier 2 (unnamed account, rule 8). Content screen before Creditor_Lines append found the TOT006 pull identical to the Batch 49 embed (proving ABN42 = TOT006) and only 24 ELE032 lines new. No green block, column T or capture field touched; control total unchanged.
- 58.0 Batch 58 capture (25-Aug-2026, v73). Two page-line corpora in one rule 16/17 build: Flavell-Dau / F82 Landscaping (59 invoices, Xero layout; the single charge is described over one to seven layout rows, joined verbatim, with the PO reference captured as a zero-amount printed row where it prints as one; the Reference field carries the CR number and the site) and Play Force Australia (35 invoices in two layouts: Xero classic with per-line park names on the inspection rounds, and the in-house Qty|Item|Description layout with a CR-site job header and printed T&C pages). EvID = printed INV-#### on both families except two Method 34.2 collisions (FD-0206 v the Play Force INV-0206; PF-5278 v the Pool Shop QLD INV-5278). Ten check-variant rows: derivation equality (7, every one also on the check-3 per-line GST basket, 2c on the four $317.30 rounds and 1c on the three repairs), the v41 check-1 per-line rounding tolerance (INV-7327, 7.50 x $105.41) and the v23 sum-tie (INV-4010, posted as ex/1.1 + GST/1.1 on two rows, Open Items #254). Thirteen Xero dates null in the corpus restated from the printed Invoice Date line (rule 19.2, page text retained). Green blocks 95 rows; EI +94; EIL +194; recon 2,570 -> 2,664; sighted 2,441 -> 2,536; variant floor 884 -> 894; GST-inclusive/tolerance allowance $41,095.38 -> $41,095.37 (the INV-7327 cent); BP:FD-P2 minted (the Payment details / ACC: wording on the earlier Flavell faces). Sites: the face outranks the CR-register attribution where they name different gazetteer parks ([CR DISAGREES] annotated on DZ); a printed head the gazetteer does not carry is held for the crosswalk (#251) and an existing CR attribution on such a row is kept. Citation limbs rewritten from the EvID maps on the recon relocation (19.10).
- 59.0 Batch 59 capture (25-Aug-2026, v74). One page-line corpus, one vendor: T & H Levai Pty Ltd, 70 invoices on the Xero template already captured at v37 and v52 (officer / contract / PK / "CR - Site (Ref: n)" / "Completed - date" above one priced line; qty and unit price not printed; GST as a rate on the totals block). Four descriptions continue on the layout row BELOW the priced row and are joined verbatim with " | ", every printed row asserted present in the retained text layer (rule 19.2). ZERO check variants - the batch is the counter-example to the variant machinery: 70 of 70 self-tie, and both faces whose subtotal lands on a half cent (INV-37611 $78.125, INV-38579 $79.245) print the half-up figure, which is exactly what Excel ROUND returns, so check 3 takes its standard form. Green blocks 70 rows; EI +70; EIL +70; recon 2,664 -> 2,734; sighted 2,536 -> 2,606; variant floor unchanged at 894; allowance unchanged at $41,095.37; BP:LEV-P1 reused (122 -> 192 sightings), no new key. Sites: 62 gazetteer, 2 by an existing user-decided Site_Crosswalk entry (the printed spacing variant "JJ Smith Park"), 6 held for the crosswalk (#257). Citation limbs rewritten from the EvID maps on the recon relocation (19.10), preserving the v73 check-1 tolerance wording on INV-7327.
- 60.0 Site decisions, the returned v74 worklist (25-Aug-2026, v75). A SITE BUILD, not a capture build: column T, the green blocks, Status, Nature, Evidence Tier and the sighted count are untouched, and the verify re-asserts every capture control unchanged. Twelve decision units applied - ten site names on 14 register rows and two non-park rulings confirming nine rows already at "No site determinable". Four decided labels were new to the gazetteer, so Panel A was extended 601 -> 605 parks: the eleven group rows, the total row, the tie-check row and the whole of Panel B were relocated four rows with their own-row references rewritten from a row map (19.10), the totals row rewritten explicitly against the new panel bounds (the self-inclusive SUM trap, 19.11), and the tie check repointed. Every decided string is recorded on Site_Crosswalk with its basis and directory link, so the same question cannot return on the next worklist.
- 61.0 Site decisions and auto-conversions (25-Aug-2026, v76). A site build: column T, the green blocks, Status, Nature, Evidence Tier and the sighted count (2,606) untouched, and the verify re-asserts the whole capture control set. Twelve decision units on 52 rows, matched to their rows by the PRINTED HEAD (col DB) exactly, or by prefix where the worklist truncated the string at 70 characters, with the line count and the value asserted against the worklist before any write. Seven distinct printed strings resolve to one park (Village Green), which is the ordinary case, not an anomaly: a suburb, a feature name in three word orders, the label plus a suffix the directory does not carry, and two supply addresses. The 14 confirmed auto-conversions were split per their own captured line amounts into Site_Allocation (+69 allocation rows), the rows moved to the allocated class, the allocation totals row rewritten explicitly and every Site_Allocation range cited on any sheet repointed to the new tail (19.11).
- 62.0 Reference-data ingestion (25-Aug-2026, v77). A site build of the narrowest kind: NO register cell was written at all, not even DY or DZ, and the build asserts that the whole DY and DZ distribution is byte-identical before and after. Column T, the green blocks, Status, Nature, Evidence Tier and the sighted count (2,606) are untouched and the verify re-asserts the entire capture control set. Four sources were ingested. The SharePoint council site master (Council_Sites, 1,056 rows) proved to be the same authority the Sites panel already relied on rather than a competing one: it agrees with the panel on the council asset number in all 438 cases where both carried one and on the division in all 485, which is why its 64 fills are applied directly rather than adjudicated. The TechOne Parks Asset Details export (Park_Assets, 431 files; Park_Asset_Items, 8,681 components) is the richer source but covers only 425 parks against 605 gazetteer labels, so it corroborates rather than supersedes; it independently confirms 34 of the 64 fills. Two traps are recorded on the sheets themselves: the master's Active column reads False on every row and is an export artefact, and Unit Cost on the component sheet is an asset carrying value, not expenditure, and must never be tied to the control total. Vendor_Asset_Leads is new and is explicitly a rule 19.9 matching aid, carried in the workbook because it is the most useful thing in the ingestion and therefore the most dangerous if mistaken for evidence: twelve manufacturers on the asset register resolve to creditors already on the register, giving 737 vendor-to-park leads over 321 parks for lines that name no site. It narrows candidates and decides nothing. The 89 unfilled gazetteer labels and the 52 pending supply points were NOT resolved by fuzzy match; both ship as worklists (Open Items #268, #269) because consolidating two labels or accepting a street-level address hit are decisions about canonical naming and about inference, not data fixes.
- 63.0 Batch 60 capture (25-Aug-2026, v78). 78 Weis Contractors invoices from WEI013.pdf, $45,129.64 ex GST. Three things in this build are worth carrying forward. FIRST, the reference collision: two printed invoice numbers in this batch are already held on Evidence_Invoices for a different supplier at a different amount. A bare reference screen reads those as re-sightings and skips them; the amount gate at 1.1 within two cents is what caught it, which is rule 19.9 earning its place. The two Weis documents take a vendor-qualified Ev ID so the rule 16b SUMIF cannot pool two suppliers under one key, and the verify now asserts Ev ID uniqueness across the whole sheet as a standing control. SECOND, the extractor left document-level invoice_date null on all 78 while the date is plainly printed in the header block. The build RESTATES it from each document's own retained page text (19.2), never guesses, and the same pass recovers due date, the Reference field, contract number, purchase order and the printed PK; the build then asserts that every one of the 290 captured line strings appears verbatim in the raw corpus before a single cell is written. A green gate proves amounts, not wording. THIRD, this template prints the work date and the site as their own zero-amount rows in the line table, above the priced line they belong to. Those 121 companion rows are captured as rows in their own right (rule 16) and are never green-blocked; on the nine multi-site invoices they are also what makes the allocation possible, each site line owning the priced lines beneath it until the next site line. Sighted 2,606 to 2,684; control total unchanged at $11,377,312.51.
- 64.0 Batch 61 capture (25-Aug-2026, v79). 38 Q Power invoices from an 89-page Q Power.pdf, $30,751.44 ex GST. The batch is unremarkable arithmetically and instructive about the extraction layer. TWO systematic defects landed on all 38 documents from the same prompt: the document-level invoice_date held the PLEASE PAY BY date, because the header prints "PLEASE PAY BY | AMOUNT | INVOICE DATE" over three values and the extractor took the first; and the document-level printed_gst was a byte copy of the subtotal, which would have failed check 3 on every row. Neither was patched around. Both were RESTATED from each document's own retained page text, and the build proves the defect before it repairs it, asserting on every document that the corpus invoice_date equals the recovered PLEASE PAY BY date and that the corpus printed_gst equals the corpus subtotal. The recovered GST is the figure printed in the totals block, never total minus subtotal: deriving it would have satisfied check 3 while breaching rule 17, and a check that passes on a derived input proves nothing. Two smaller points. Seven priced rows print no Item text and their page returned no OCR-readable text, so they are captured as blank as printed with quantity, unit price, GST rate and amount all present, and the blank is named in the anomalies note rather than being quietly filled from the description block. And fifteen invoices state no PK anywhere on the printed face while the ledger charges all fifteen to PK000412; the green block records the PK as not printed, because carrying the ledger value into a capture field is exactly what rule 17 and 19.9 forbid. Sighted 2,684 to 2,722; control total unchanged at $11,377,312.51.
- 65.0 Batch 63 capture (25-Aug-2026, v83). A 25-page mixed-supplier creditor-attachment bundle (C00227208.pdf) yielding 12 captures across five suppliers, $7,933.45 ex GST, and three points of method. FIRST, the Paintback levy: four PPG invoices print a statutory levy ex GST in the totals block, the ledger expenses PRODUCT TOTAL + LEVY, and the printed GST is 10% of that same base, so strict check 3 misses by 6 to 12 cents, beyond the rounding-tolerance variant. A new check 3 LEVY BASIS variant is introduced (Open Item #281, to be ratified), built only from printed figures with the levy hardcoded in the formula the way recon targets already are; check 2 runs on the ratified derivation-equality form. The levy is NOT an item line and never enters the line reconciliation. SECOND, a priceless item grid defeats rule 16(b): Hills Fencing prints quantities and descriptions with no line prices, so both invoices are HELD with the sighting recorded in the coding note and the priced docket requested, rather than fabricating a summary line, which rule 16 names a critical failure. THIRD, capture supersedes identification cleanly: ten rows identified Tier 2 at v82 this morning are upgraded by sight this afternoon, and each coding note says so. One split posting (Xzibit FP-6501, $1,043.64 + a 36-cent "variance" row) ties under the v23 SUM-TIE variant. Sighted 2,722 to 2,735; control total unchanged at $11,377,312.51.
- 66.0 Batch 64 capture (26-Aug-2026, v84). A 54-page binder named for one supplier that holds three (T & H Levai 41 faces, T2 Electrical & Data 5, Play Force Australia 4), 50 captures, $29,744.75 ex GST, 55 printed lines, every row Confirmed, and four points of method. FIRST, the extractor repairs: the T2 header band put the PLEASE PAY BY date in invoice_date on every T2 face (F1 form 4) and the subtotal in printed_gst (F12 field bleed), and the Levai Xero face put the subtotal in printed_total_incl_gst on all 41 (F1 form 3); all three were restated by label from the retained text layer, never derived, and every restated figure satisfies Subtotal + GST = Total on its own face. SECOND, the single-scope route: T2 3222869 prints a Description block and totals with no priced grid, so it is captured as one line at the printed Sub-Total ex GST under the v40 BLC / v51 Q Power precedent, superseding a HELD recorded in an earlier, unbuilt brief; the invoice has one scope and this is not a summary row. THIRD, one check-1 per-line rounding tolerance (Play Force INV-6938, 0.50 x $105.41) moves the GST-inclusive allowance down one cent to $41,095.36, restated in the control label and formula. FOURTH, citation drift was live again on both limbs (register column 28 stale by 411 rows everywhere; Evidence_Invoices column 13 stale on all but the v83 rows) because the v78, v79 and v83 relocations propagated neither limb and their verifies did not audit them; both limbs are rewritten from the EvID map here and the verify audits every citation (Open Item #291). Site dimension: 45 single printed sites written, three through NEW Site_Crosswalk rows (v61 suburb rule twice, v55 address-suffix repair once, corroborated by Council_Sites), five heads to harvest (#287), one CR-register disagreement resolved in favour of the face. Sighted 2,735 to 2,785; control total unchanged at $11,377,312.51. Housekeeping: the stale Config keys EIL_DATA, RECON_ROWS, RECON_COUNT and EI_CONTROL_ROWS (frozen at v74 values while EIL_END, EIL_RECON and EI_CONTROLS moved) are restated to the live positions.
- 67.0 Batch 65 capture (26-Aug-2026, v85). Harpley 3.pdf, 166 pages, 84 runs, 82 unique invoices, $40,132.21 ex GST, 198 printed rows, every row Confirmed. Template A throughout (Kingston letterhead, MYOB "How to pay" trailer page): header fields read by LABEL (the CR/WO# and Work Description labels print with and without a following space, and dates print with one- or two-digit days, so the label token is asserted as printed and dates are normalised on write); the printed GST is a bare $ line under "Sub Total:" and is restated and re-tied on every face. Two grid rows print a Materials narrative with no quantity or extension: captured at 0.00 with nothing inferred (rule 16a), the printed Sub Total ties, and the question goes to Harpley (#294). One PK divergence (#293) and one face printing two work orders. The rule 12 EvID screen keeps leading zeros, which is why the eight Harpley/Q Power reference collisions the brief warned of never bit (#297). Site dimension: 64 single sites (60 gazetteer, 3 existing crosswalk, 1 new spelling canonicalisation), 18 heads to harvest (#296), none with a CR-register attribution to keep. Both citation limbs moved +198 with the recon block and were rewritten (19.10). Sighted 2,785 to 2,867; control total unchanged at $11,377,312.51.
- 68.0 Batch 66 capture (26-Aug-2026, v86). The PLA073.pdf / T2.pdf pair whose brief arrived a session early: 15 documents, of which nine had already been captured from binder 3 at v84, so the rule 12 EvID screen (prefix-aware, on the corpus before any parse) reduced the batch to six Play Force faces, $751.73 ex GST, 9 printed lines, every row Confirmed, every site a gazetteer label agreeing with the CR register. The method point is the screen itself: a duplicate screen that runs on the corpus, not only on the upload md5, is what stops a second binder from re-capturing lines under an EvID that already pools them (the v40 rule, third application). Six zero-amount same-document companions are cross-referenced and never green-blocked. Sighted 2,867 to 2,873; control total unchanged at $11,377,312.51.
- 69.0 Batch 68 identification (26-Aug-2026, v87). Three creditor histories in a new 16-column vintage (Ageing and Source columns appended; the standing read-by-name rule absorbed it without change), 1280 ledger rows appended verbatim, 17 lines identified Tier 2 for $7,404.10 ex GST. Two points. FIRST, the export prints its own totals row as a trailing row with a blank document type; it is excluded from the append and used as the tie for the rows above it. SECOND, the Higgins history is a study in what the amount gate refuses: 42 references corroborate rows already labelled Higgins, and the 38 that fall in the Unidentified 9-digit series all carry $0.00 on the register, so none can be corroborated and none is written. The proposed series-exclusive zero-amount variant is restated on #301 for a decision at the next instructions revision, not applied here. Control total unchanged at $11,377,312.51; sighted count unchanged at 2,873.
- 65.0 v88 SITE BUILD (26-Aug-2026) - the address route closes on Council parcel records. Twenty-four IntraMaps Property Reports (Property Report / Customer Service Report / Planning Scheme Report PDFs plus one property CSV) were supplied by the user on 26-Aug-2026. Eight parcels decide 14 printed supply strings covering 374 register lines and $10,218.76. The test is NUMBER-IN-RANGE plus suburb: the printed house number must fall inside the parcel address range and the printed suburb must match (prefix-matched, because register column 42 truncates at forty characters). Street-only matching remains banned - the five numberless strings that hit a supplied parcel were NOT written. Lot references ("Lot 3", "Lot 951") are plan identifiers and are excluded from house-number extraction; reading them as street numbers wrongly rejected the largest supply point on the first pass. Column T, the green block, Status, Nature, Evidence Tier and the sighted count (2,873) are untouched and the verify carries the entire prior capture control set.
- 65.1 Two structural gazetteer additions were unavoidable and both are DISAMBIGUATIONS, not spellings. "Anzac Park (Logan Village)" is a different park from the gazetteer's "Anzac Park" (Alba Lane, Kingston), and "Settlers Park (Flagstone)" is a different park from "Settlers Park" (Nurdi Street, Woodridge). Writing the bare label in either case would have attributed the spend to the wrong park in the wrong division. Panel A grows 605 -> 607, the eleven group rows, the total row, the tie check and the whole of Panel B relocate two rows with their own-row references rewritten, and the totals row is rewritten explicitly against the new bounds.
- 66.0 v89 CAPTURE BUILD, Batch 67 (Secure Entry binder: Q Power + Secure Entry Group) (26-Aug-2026). Forty-six pages, fifteen documents, two suppliers. Eight Q Power invoices captured to rules 16 and 17: 15 printed lines, $2,975.00 ex GST, three live checks TRUE on every one, sighted 2,873 -> 2,881. Seven Secure Entry Group invoices HELD: their item grid prints quantities with NO item prices and no ex-GST subtotal, so rule 16(b) cannot tie and rule 17 check 1 is unsatisfiable. The v83 Hills Fencing precedent applies unchanged: sighting, supplier, printed ABN and page range recorded in the coding note, NO EvID and no green block. Their register amounts equal printed total less printed GST, which is a DERIVATION and is never captured as a printed subtotal.
- 66.1 Both v79 Q Power extractor defects recurred on all eight documents and both were PROVEN before repair, so a corrected corpus fails the assertion rather than being silently repaired. (1) invoice_date carried the PLEASE PAY BY value: the header prints PLEASE PAY BY | AMOUNT | INVOICE DATE and the extractor took the first of three. (2) printed_gst was a byte copy of printed_subtotal_ex_gst on 8/8, so a 10% check would have compared a number with itself. The true GST is recovered VERBATIM from each invoice's own totals block and a regex confirms the recovered string appears in that document's retained page text. Standing rule: a check that passes on a derived input proves nothing. Open Item #276 is upgraded from a v79 observation to a persistent extractor class.
- 66.2 REFERENCE COLLISION, second batch running. Q Power 14930 ($370.00) collides with EvID 00014930, a Harpley Services invoice at $510.00 already Confirmed on Register row 3526; 14509 collides the same way with Harpley 00014509 ($180.00) on row 7595. Both were separated on the amount gate. A bare reference screen would have read each as a re-sighting and skipped it. Rule 19.9 holds: matching aids are not evidence, and the amount must be part of the match key wherever a zero-padded creditor reference series overlaps a bare one.
- 67.0 v90 BUILD, Batch 68 (Mix.pdf corpus: STAR CARPENTRY + Harpley Services; plus five Logan Plumbing creditor PDFs) (28-Aug-2026). 80 invoices captured to rules 16 and 17: 161 printed lines, $39,677.75 ex GST, three live checks TRUE on every one. Sighted 2,881 -> 2,961. Three suppliers: STAR CARPENTRY (46), Harpley Services (29), Logan Plumbing Service (5). Logan Plumbing moves from ONE sighted line in 209 to six. Control total $11,377,312.51 unchanged; column T never written.
- 67.1 ONE INVOICE HELD, and it is a rule 7 finding. STAR CARPENTRY INV-9601 prints subtotal $653.00, GST $14.30 and total $667.30. The GST is exactly 10% of the $143.00 materials line and nil on the $510.00 labour line, so the printed total is only partly GST-bearing. The register carries $606.64 = $667.30 / 1.1, which is the Hilltops pattern rule 7 names as an error. Checks 2 and 3 both return FALSE. The ratified check-2 derivation-equality variant is NOT applied: it exists for the 1-2c TechOne artefact, and using it here would launder a $46.36 understatement into a Confirmed line. Held pending a coding decision (Open Item #315).
- 67.2 CORPUS REPAIR, one class, 80/81 documents. invoice_date was null on every document in corpus_mix.json. Every printed header field on every green block in this batch - invoice date, due date, order number, CR/WO, PK, site head, work description, requesting officer - is restated VERBATIM from that document's own retained page text under rule 19.2, never from the register and never guessed. Two documents (INV-9823, INV-9910) print no site head at all and are recorded as such rather than filled from the ledger.
- 67.3 LOGAN PLUMBING PAGE-2 TRAP. The five creditor PDFs continue their priced grid on page 2, after the PAYMENT ADVICE block. A page-1-only read of 20980 captures one line at $190.00 against a printed subtotal of $983.75 and reconciles $793.75 short. Standing rule: read the whole document before declaring a rule 16(b) failure.
- 67.4 SITE ATTRIBUTION from two IntraMaps parcel batches, 198 register rows. 51 Council parcels are now held in the index. Three parcels resolved NEGATIVELY: 1 and 67 Carl Heck Boulevard and 125 City Road are PRIVATELY owned, so no park attribution is available on them. A decisive negative is a result, not a gap. A further 2 rows are resolved on the parcel record but NOT written: Roselea Park and Bilubera Park are absent from Sites Panel A, and a DY label Panel A does not carry would break the COUNTIF key (Open Item #322).
- 68.0 v91 CAPTURE BUILD, Batch 69 (T & H LEVAI binder "mix 2.pdf", 40 pages) (28-Aug-2026). 39 T & H Levai invoices captured to rules 16 and 17: 39 printed lines, $17,912.37 ex GST, three live checks TRUE on every one. Sighted 2,961 -> 3,000. This is the first bulk sighting of LEV002, the largest single unconfirmed block in the register. Control total $11,377,312.51 unchanged; column T never written.
- 68.1 DUPLICATE COPY, rule 13. INV-38202 ($480.00) is printed twice in the binder, on pages 17 and 18. The extractor recorded page 18 in full as DUPLICATE_COPY with null arithmetic and duplicate_of pointing at page 17. It is NOT re-captured: one EvID, one register line, one set of printed lines. The binder therefore holds 40 physical documents and 39 unique invoices, and the capture report's "40 documents" is a page count, not an invoice count.
- 68.2 SITE ATTRIBUTION COMES FREE ON THIS TEMPLATE. The Levai invoice face prints the requesting officer, the contract, the PK and a Customer Request number followed by the park name, so 34 of 39 resolve straight to a Sites Panel A label off their own face. Three more resolve to a Council site register entry that Panel A does not carry (Christopher Park, Wendt Park, Homestead Park (Flagstone)) and are held under Open Item #322. Two do not resolve: "Exilis Street" is a street with two Council candidates, and "Settlers Park - Jimboomba" names a suburb that matches no Settlers Park in the Council register.
- 69.0 v92 BUILD, Batch 70 (Mix 3.pdf: BrizSouth Locksmiths, Bunnings trade, 4Park) + Origin sub-account breakdown (28-Aug-2026). 29 invoices captured to rules 16 and 17 across three suppliers - BrizSouth Locksmiths 17, Bunnings trade 8, 4Park 4: 103 printed lines, $5,455.23 ex GST, three live checks TRUE on every one. Sighted 3,000 -> 3,029. Control total $11,377,312.51 unchanged; column T never written.
- 69.1 SIX DOUBLE-RENDERED PAGES, a new extractor trap. On BrizSouth pages C12995, C13037, C13092, C13093, C13104 and C13122 the PDF text layer draws every content line TWICE, the second copy kerning-damaged ("S u p p l y", "M L 3 0 0", "Labournote:"). The extractor summed the doubled priced lines and returned EXACTLY twice the printed subtotal on all six. The duplication is provable without the source PDF: SUBTOTAL and PLUS 10% GST each appear twice in the text stream and once in the OCR stream. Capture is restated from each page's own OCR stream, whose priced lines reconcile to the printed subtotal to the cent and whose SUBTOTAL, GST and TOTAL agree with the text layer. Standing rule: where a text layer is double-rendered and the OCR stream is not, the OCR stream is the better read - but only where a figure present in BOTH streams proves the repair.
- 69.2 TWO ZERO-CAPTURE PAGES. 4Park INV101780 and INV105798 returned captured_ex_gst 0.00 against printed subtotals of $338.00 and $95.80. The priced grid is present in the retained text; the extractor typed it NARRATIVE so no PRICED line was emitted. The grid prints quantity, unit price, GST and GST-inclusive amount, and the ex-GST extension is the printed inclusive amount less the printed GST, both read from the page. INV105798 carried a SECOND defect: printed_gst was 95.80, a byte copy of the "Total AUD Excl. GST" line. The invoice prints "10% GST 9.58" on both streams. Open Item #276 recurs for the third batch running.
- 69.3 CHECK-3 ROUNDING TOLERANCE, five Bunnings baskets. Bunnings computes GST per printed line and sums, so the basket GST differs from ROUND(subtotal x 0.1, 2) by one cent on 8066/01258115, 8066/01273867, 8171/01434719, 8171/01482870 and 8171/01515573. The ratified check-3 rounding-tolerance variant applies. The SAME five also need the ratified check-2 one-cent tolerance: TechOne derived each register amount as the printed total incl GST divided by 1.1, which lands a cent from the printed subtotal - the rule 7 artefact, not the Hilltops error, because these totals are fully GST-bearing. Both checks are written as ROUND(ABS(...),2)<=0.01 and return the literal TRUE, tagged [check variant]. Check 1 ties to the cent on all five, so the line capture is exact and only the derivations round.
- 69.4 ORIGIN SUB-ACCOUNT BREAKDOWN, the answer to a 40-character field. Nine breakdown CSVs (archive md5 2bec9afe79842fe8548563c5693ef230) carry 842 Origin sub-accounts and 751 distinct SERVICE ADDRESSES. Register column 42 is a hard 40-character TechOne field, so every long supply-point string in the register is truncated. The breakdown de-truncates 27 of the 34 printed strings still on the address-pending route, written to 603 register rows as EVIDENCE in the coding note. No park is attributed on this build: several de-truncated addresses now show exactly one Council park on their street, but the number range still has to be tested (Flesser Reserve is 278-326 Flesser Road and the supply point is 150, which EXCLUDES it). That adjudication is a site build, not a capture build.
- 70.0 v93 build (28-Aug-2026): Batch 71, two corpora. mix_batch delivered as a raw-text dump (53 priced records of 13,652); repaired in-session: labels re-scanned with total-inc-gst before generic gst (mislabel trap), priced rows recovered from the retained layout lines, Q Power ex-GST derived from the page-1 AMOUNT incl/1.1 (page-2 totals right-margin truncated) and captured as a single lump-sum line per the QP-14208 precedent, tagged [check variant] (17 rows). mix_33 (Flavell-Dau) 58/58 green; dates restated (Xero header split). Vendor-aware duplicate screen mandatory: a leading-zero-normalised screen falsely matched 10 Harpley 00014xxx numbers onto Q Power 14xxx EvIDs. All 187 EI col-13 citations written from the EvID maps; both limbs re-audited across all 3,347 rows.
- 71.0 v94 build (31-Aug-2026), taxonomy v3 and journal provenance | Three axes on every register line. Line Kind (col ED) is what sort of ledger event it is, computed on EVERY line rather than only on lines falling past the account step; this is the untagged accounting-event fix. Charge Source (col EF) is who billed Council. Theme (cols EA/EB) is what work was done, assigned by the ratified precedence P1 account governs, P2 sighted invoice at job level by dominant printed-line value, P3 single-scope vendor, P4 narration single class only, P5 stores, P6 declared evidence state. Level 1 is a live INDEX/MATCH into Theme_Map_v3 (rule 4). Journal provenance (cols EK to EP) carries the journal reference as an analytical key: because the register loads only the legs touching 4090240 and 4090260, a reference netting non-zero has its counterparty in a cost centre this register cannot see. A set key binds mirror pairs and within-reference repeats into one economic event. Not built: the level-3 sub-category layer and the level-1 rename to Council service lines, both per the structure of record.
- 72.0 v95 build (31-Aug-2026), self-containment correction | Rule 14 requires the workbook to stand alone. v94 carried the three axes as register columns and the reference-level journal sheets, but the worklists a reader must ACT on, the journals to pull and the unpaired accruals, existed only in the derived analysis workbook. A reader holding the register alone could see that 227 journal references do not balance but could not see which 64 to request from TechOne. v95 folds in Priority_Pull and Unpaired_Accruals, and the two axis summaries Line_Kind and Charge_Source, plus Evidence_Coverage which states what each theme figure rests on. Every figure on all five sheets is a live SUMIFS, SUMIF or COUNTIF over the Register, so none of them can drift from it.
- 73.0 v96 build (31-Aug-2026), assessed scope and the reversing-journal test | Two control totals now operate and they must not be confused. The LOADED total, $11,377,312.51, is what Register column T sums to across four financial years and is the invariant of rule 10. The ASSESSED total, $10,537,981.98, is the three financial years under assessment, FY2023/24 to FY2025/26, and is what every share, trend and thematic total is taken on. FY2026/27 is loaded, ties its extract, and appears as a memo column; it is excluded from analysis because the internal charging change and the PK fragmentation in that year make it non-comparable (rule 11). Register col EM carries the scope on every line. Separately, the reversing-journal test: all 77 RJ references net to exactly zero, so the accrual residue is entirely in manual journals. DocType does not identify reversing journals, the reference prefix does; col EN carries the type.
- 74.0 v97 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch 72 under the rule 20 driver | First build run through pswp_build_batch.py, the brief-driven driver required by project instructions v10 rule 20. Positions are read from Config and every column letter is asserted against its index before a formula uses it. Batch facts, being the vendors, the boilerplate keys, the categories, the held sites, the Open Items and the control pins, come from a batch brief JSON authored by the session; the script contains no batch content. Pre-write gates: md5 against Data_Acquisition, a vendor-aware EvID duplicate screen, the three arithmetic limbs computed in Decimal with ROUND_HALF_UP, 1,372 five-word shingle assertions of every captured field against the retained page text, and a match gate requiring exactly one unclaimed AP register row per document at the exact printed subtotal. All passed. Sixty-one register rows moved from Partial to Confirmed. Nature Category is assigned from each row's own sighted lines, testing cleaning, then structure, then plumbing, and falling back to the printed work description only where every priced line is a bare attendance date; that sequencing matters, because reading the object of the work off the word toilet alone miscodes bollards behind a toilet block and door catches on a toilet block as plumbing. The v3 theme columns move to P2 sighted job, dominant scope on 60 rows; the one row on account 73111 keeps its ratified P1 assignment because P1 is system record governs, no judgement.
- 75.0 v98 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch 73 and four driver extensions | Second build under the rule 20 driver, and the batch that forced the driver to grow. Four mechanics were added rather than written ad hoc (rule 19.1): a batch may now declare its site decisions per invoice in the brief, with the driver refusing any decision whose named site is not a Sites panel A label; new Vendor_Boilerplate keys may be created, not only incremented; Site_Crosswalk decisions may be added; and rows may be inserted into the Evidence_Invoices tail so correspondence evidence lands between the totals block and the controls without disturbing either. Two extractor defects were repaired from the retained page text: the Xero template's TOTAL GST line is typed PRICED, which would otherwise force a check-1 incl-GST variant on both Flavell-Dau invoices, and the Traffic Control Supplies zero-amount item rows are typed NARRATIVE, which would otherwise drop seven printed lines in breach of rule 16(b). Neither invoice needs a check variant once repaired. The verbatim gate ran 430 five-word shingle assertions with no failure, and it caught a live parser defect on the way: a case-insensitive search for 'Our Contact:' matches inside 'Your Contact:', silently capturing the requesting officer as the site contact.
- 76.0 v99 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch 74, the cleanest binder yet and one that needed no new mechanic | Eighty-seven invoices on a single vendor, a single contract, a single purchase order and a single account. Every document tied on all three limbs at the first attempt, 2,256 five-word shingle assertions passed with no failure, and the batch-1 Harpley template parser handled the binder unchanged: the first reuse of a shipped parser across two batches, which is the point of shipping them. The value of the batch is not the $19,357.36 it captures but the site evidence it carries: 87 invoices each naming a park on the invoice face, 73 of which resolve to the gazetteer and lift those register lines from an inferred site to a printed one. Three findings needed judgement rather than code. Invoice 00014948 prints PK 000118 where the other 86 print PK 000415 and the ledger charges PK000415 on all 87; the printed PK is captured and the charge is left alone (rule 1). Invoice 00014994 prints a blank Site Details, so column DY is left empty rather than inferred from the GIS layer, which the standing rule forbids. The purchase order prints its contract reference as LCC09B2022 while all 87 invoices print LCC-09-2022; both forms are captured as printed and neither is normalised.
- 77.0 v100 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch 75, the first batch aimed at the unknown pocket | Forty-three T & H Levai invoices, one priced line each, every one of them landing on a register line that read 'No invoice sighted' under P6. That is the distinction v99 made explicit and this build acts on: capture aimed at P1 or P3 lines buys evidence grade, capture aimed at P6 lines changes what the register says. The batch also exercised the shipped Levai parser for the first time outside its own batch, and found one defect worth recording: the parser matched a face code on the pattern PK followed by digits, so the mis-typed PL000118 on INV-38302 fell through to the site block instead. The pattern is now P, any letter, six digits, with the printed form retained verbatim and the normalised PK derived on join under rule 13. Four judgements were needed and none of them is a coding change: two face codes disagree with the ledger charge and are raised rather than adjusted (rule 1); one face code is a printed typo that normalises cleanly; one contract reference prints PAR/335L/2026 where the vendor's other invoices print PAR/335L/2023, the second contract-reference anomaly on this vendor after INV-39072 in Batch 72. The binder itself is the finding of record: 43 invoice faces and no work orders, photographs, quotes, attendance sheets or completion records, which is sufficient for rule 17 where the invoice narrates completed works and insufficient on INV-37748, where it states the work was already completed by others and charges $350.00 anyway.
- 78.0 v101 build (31-Aug-2026), journal source documents | Rule 17 does not reach journals; they rest on pairing (rule 6). A journal reference that does not balance inside the PS/WP scope has, until now, been evidenced only by its ledger narration. This build introduces the source document as the pairing evidence: the TechOne GENJNL Document Line Table for the whole journal, embedded verbatim on Journal_Sources, with two live controls per reference, that the document nets to zero and that its in-scope leg ties the register lines to the cent. Both pass on all four. The Nature Basis "Journal source document (GENJNL Document Line Table)" is new and it outranks "Journal narration / pairing". The build also records the trap the documents exposed: THE LEDGER NARRATION NAMES THE ORIGINAL CODING OF A JOB, NOT THE ORIGIN PK OF THE JOURNAL. GJ079210 narrates "Condition assessment (PK000511)" and "Timber Treatment (PK000510)" while its actual origin is PK000035. Reading the narration as provenance would have produced the wrong answer on every one of these lines, and it is what made the timber-treatment duplication invisible for four builds.
- 79.0 v102 build (31-Aug-2026), journal source documents, second pass | The in-scope control on an RJ accrual document is stated against the register lines the document MATCHES, not against the reference net: an RJ reference carries both the accrual and its reversal, so its net is always zero while the document shows one side only. All four RJ documents tie exactly, and the unmatched remainder is in every case the exact negative, carried as Accrual reversal. Second standing note: where a supplier invoice travels with the journal, the invoice is NOT captured in a journal-source build. Rule 16 capture requires the line schedule to reconcile to the printed subtotal to the cent, and the capture write shifts the Evidence_Invoice_Lines reconciliation block, which triggers the rule 19.10 citation-limb rewrite. Both belong in a capture build.
- 80.0 v103 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch batch capture and the first citation-limb relocation since v92 | Three standing notes. First, Secure Entry Group invoices print no ex GST item amounts and no ex GST subtotal; ex GST is captured as printed total less printed GST and the ratified check 1 incl-GST basis variant is tagged on each. Second, an invoice that reaches PS/WP only through a journal has no AP register line and therefore cannot carry a rule 17 green block; it is captured under rule 16 alone and marked STAGED on Evidence_Invoices, following the pre-v4 out-of-scope Origin precedent. Third, the Evidence_Invoice_Lines reconciliation block moved 62 rows at this build. Both citation limbs were rewritten from the EvID to recon-row map, never by positional shift, and the verify pass audits both against the block.
- 81.0 v104 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch 33 Levai capture and the garbage-charges journal source embed | Four standing notes. First, the batch33 corpus carried the printed subtotal in the header total field on all 47 documents; under rule 19.2 the Total was restated from the retained printed Total line, never derived, and on all 47 the printed Total equals subtotal plus GST with Balance Due agreeing. Second, the Levai Xero layout prints no per-line qty, unit price or GST; those fields are recorded (not printed) and the doc-level GST is captured at the invoice level only. Third, the invoice face outranks the Customer Request register location join for site attribution (INV-38809, Federation Drive Reserve over Riedel Park); agreement upgrades the basis, disagreement rewrites the site and raises an Open Item. Fourth, a sighted park absent from Sites Panel A is routed to the candidate-harvest group rather than written to column DY, so the Panel A whole-register tie holds; the park joins the Panel A extension queue (Open Item #385).
- 82.0 v105 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch 34 raw-PDF capture route and the freight-inclusive capture target. Four standing notes. First, TechOne creditor attachment PDFs were captured on the rule 19.2 raw-text fallback (pdftotext -layout, page text retained in the workbook at column DP); image-borne letterhead ABNs were recovered by targeted tesseract OCR and cross-proofed against the text layer (BrizSouth OCR cross-proof route). Second, where a supplier prints freight separately and the register carries the freight-inclusive ex GST amount, the capture target is Sub Total plus Freight as printed, freight is captured as its own line (established treatment, 25 precedent lines), and the basis is documented in the anomalies column; the printed GST proving the combined base (10% exactly) is a required part of the note. Third, an invoice silent on the usage site neither confirms nor supersedes an existing GL-narration site attribution. Fourth, the batch driver gained a brief-driven site_writes mechanic (DY/DZ; a DZ group label must match the Sites Panel A group row text exactly) and now refreshes plain-number control labels; EI data 5:3624 (totals 3626, controls 3637:3650); EIL 5:8683; recon 8687:12306 (3,620 rows); both citation sets rewritten from the EvID maps.
- 83.0 v106 build (31-Aug-2026), the FY2025/26 quarantine decomposition | The two FY2025/26 Line_Kind cells (+$76,415.14 accrual-reversal net; -$131,489.96 recode net) are decomposed on a live, controlled panel beneath the grid. Mechanics: eight journal source documents embedded with nets-to-zero and in-scope-leg controls (in-scope flags set by leg-level matching of every register line to its document leg, PK-ledger by PK and amount, SL-ledger by natural account and amount); cross-document set keys written to Register column EL and mirror references to EK (the per-reference SET-<ref> keys of the v3 build are superseded on member rows); the raw grid cells are never altered - the panel reads them live and two controls tie each decomposition to its cell. Standing findings: an EOY accrual pair straddles the FY boundary by construction and is read as one set, never netted within a year; a duplicate posting and its correction (GJ072622 x2, GJ075853) are a cross-FY set whose true cost is the corrected net; a recode implementing a sighted invoice breakdown (Pool Shop splits) is a permanent re-attribution, not noise; and the loaded 26SLACT extract predates the period-13 per-invoice accrual layer (Open Items #388), so FY2025/26 loaded totals are pre-period-13 readings.
- 84.0 v107 build (31-Aug-2026), Batch 35 and the levy-inclusive capture target. Three standing notes. First, a corpus that returns no PRICED lines is repairable in place where its TEXT and OCR layers are complete: the session rebuilds priced lines and document boundaries from the retained page text under rule 19.2, re-segmenting on the printed invoice header rather than on page pairs, and never derives a value the page does not print. Second, PPG prints PRODUCT TOTAL, FREIGHT, PAINTBACK LEVY, GST and INVOICE TOTAL in that order; the register carries the levy-inclusive ex GST figure, so the capture target is PRODUCT TOTAL plus FREIGHT plus PAINTBACK LEVY, the levy is captured as its own line and the printed GST proving the combined base is a required part of the note. This is the same family as the GX Outdoors freight-inclusive basis of v105 and needs no variant tag. Third, Bunnings prints GST per line and totals those printed figures, so the printed GST total can sit one to three cents from ten per cent of the printed subtotal; that is the ratified check 3 rounding basket, tested against the sum of the printed per-line GST rather than against the subtotal. EI data 5:3661 (totals 3663, controls 3674:3687); EIL 5:8782; recon 8786:12442 (3,657 rows); both citation sets rewritten from the EvID maps.
- 85.0 v108 build (1-Sep-2026), the group 9 re-theme pass | Rule P6 declared evidence state is a placeholder, not a verdict, and it was never refreshed when a capture build landed: rows carrying a full rule 17 green block were still declaring "No invoice sighted". The re-theme pass restates theme category from each row's own captured Evidence_Invoice_Lines descriptions, weighted by printed line value, which is rule P2 applied after the fact. Three mechanics make it safe. First, lines that carry no asset signal (labour, freight, delivery, dump fees, machine hire, call-out fees, progress payments, bare visit dates) are NEUTRAL: they are excluded from the denominator rather than counted as doubt, because a labour line belongs to whatever the material lines on the same invoice name. Second, where every captured line is neutral the fallback is the row's OWN Nature Detail then its GL narration, never another row and never a vendor default, because these suppliers do many different kinds of work and a vendor-scope default would be a fiction. Third, a category is written only where one class holds at least 60 per cent of the classifiable value; everything else holds and is listed on Open Item #394. Column T, Nature Category, the green block and the amount are untouched: this pass moves the theme axis only.
- 86.0 v109 build (1-Sep-2026), user-adjudicated standing work class | A standing order prints the same generic narration on every document, so the theme engine can only reach P6. Where the Parks Branch financial analyst identifies the standing work from local knowledge, that adjudication is recorded as a work-axis assignment under a P4 variant, "P4 narration, single class (user-adjudicated standing work)", with the adjudication stated on the row. The boundary is strict and stated on every row touched: local knowledge settles WHAT the work was, never WHETHER the line is confirmed. Status stays Partial, Nature Basis stays vendor inference, the green block stays empty and no rule 17 check is claimed; a sighted invoice still supersedes the adjudication on both axes when it lands.
- 87.0 v110 build (1-Sep-2026), Batch 36. The Levai Xero template has now delivered the same F1 fault three times running (batch 33, the qpower_arvo rebuild and this batch): the extractor reports the printed subtotal in printed_total_incl_gst because the printed Total line types as NARRATIVE. The repair is mechanical and must be applied before any use: restate the Total from the retained printed Total line, never derive it, and prove subtotal plus GST equals the restated Total on every document. A vendor whose template has failed the same way three times should be gated on that field by default. EI data 5:3731 (totals 3733, controls 3744:3757); EIL 5:8859; recon 8863:12589 (3,727 rows); both citation sets rewritten from the EvID maps.
- 88.0 v112 build (1-Sep-2026), reclassification, correspondence and the ratified treatment decisions | Three things are settled here. First, GJ076924 is reclassified from Operational to Recode / transfer journal on sighted correspondence: three of its legs tie to the cent to Pool Shop invoices receipted wholly to PK000022 and split out by request, and a fourth corrects a Harpley invoice charged to the wrong PK. A journal narrated "move cost to correct cost code" is a recode whatever the Line Kind engine inferred from its account. Second, seventeen journal-request emails are embedded verbatim on a new Correspondence sheet under rule 16d, with the operative instruction only and a source md5 per record, so the finding survives the source being discarded (rule 15). Third, the rule 5 quarantine is now READ by ratified decision rather than left open: Register column EO carries D1 permanent re-attribution, D2 prior-year adjustment or D3 structural cross-FY accrual on every affected line, and the Treatment_Decisions panel computes the effect live. No line amount changes and the assessed control total stays $10,537,981.98. The standing lesson: a quarantine line is closed by deciding how to read it, not by pulling more evidence, once every component is evidenced and set-keyed.
- 89.0 v113 build (1-Sep-2026), taxonomy review and axis 4 | The taxonomy was tested for a third theme layer and the answer is no, not yet. A work-driver layer (planned, reactive, vandalism, compliance, renewal) is the right cut for a Parks manager but leaves 57.9 per cent of assessed operational value unclassified on current evidence, and only 25.5 per cent of sighted rows classify from their captured invoice lines. Shipping it would recreate the group 9 defect one level down. What IS classifiable is the procurement route, because it comes from structured fields already on the line rather than from free text: contract reference, standing-order narration, document type and account prefix place 17,073 of 17,082 assessed operational lines. That is adopted as AXIS 4 on Register column EP with its own sheet, and it is deliberately not theme level 3, because how the work was bought is not what the work was. The standing test for any new layer: if it cannot place 90 per cent of value from evidence already held, it is a research question, not a taxonomy.
- 90.0 v114 build (1-Sep-2026), the Site_Geo coordinate table | Coordinates now live in the workbook rather than only inside a map payload, on a new Site_Geo sheet keyed on the park name as it appears on the register line. The map build reads Site_Geo first and falls back to the coordinates carried forward from the previous payload, so a coordinate supplied once is never lost when the map is rebuilt, which is what happened when build_map_v92.py went missing. Three standing rules. A coordinate is a LOCATION FACT and is recorded with its source, never silently: decimal supplied, DMS supplied, geocoded, or address route. Every point is validated against the Logan LGA envelope before it is written, and a point outside it is rejected rather than plotted, which is how the Quandong Park conflict surfaced. A street or locality centroid is acceptable for a spend map but is recorded as such, because a marker on a road frontage will later be mistaken for a parcel centroid if the basis is not stated.
- 91.0 v115 build (2-Sep-2026), FY2026/27 memo-year listing refresh | Three things are settled here. First, PK_Listing is now a P1-P3 listing for Park Services and carries the extract line count alongside the live loaded count, so the sheet states its own coverage gap rather than implying completeness; the two Water Parks rows are marked "(not re-pulled)" because that section was not in the 2-Sep-2026 extract. Second, an extract that omits Transaction Amount and Document Unique ID is not a load candidate at any level of effort: rule 9 has no LineKey to build and rule 10 has no subtotal to tie, and no amount may be derived from SE2 account totals and written to a register line, because SE2 is an aggregate and the register is a line-item record. The extract is therefore used only where it is authoritative on its own terms, being the PK / cost-centre pair universe and the per-account line counts. Third, the memo-year gap is now measured rather than asserted: SE2_Budget panel 3 reconciles every one of the 40 SE2 accounts against a live SUMIFS over the Register, ties both sides to their own controls and pins the difference at $352,231.80 across 390 lines. Cosmetic repair in the same pass: the PK_Listing line-count column carried the money number format at v114 and now carries #,##0.
- 92.0 v116 build (2-Sep-2026), the FY2025/26 section-wide population, staged | The decisive finding is structural, not financial. The register's analysis layer is keyed on financial year, not on the Assessment scope column: only 246 of 7,189 Register-citing formulas cite EM. The scope column was introduced for the FY2026/27 memo year, which is separated by its own FY value and so never needed the criterion; a second out-of-scope block INSIDE an existing financial year has no such protection. That is why this population is staged on its own sheet rather than loaded with a scope flag as originally proposed: the flag would have been written and then ignored by 96.6%% of the analysis. The order of work is therefore retrofit, verify, load, restate, and not load first. Second point, on identification: 345 staged AP lines carry a reference that matches an identified vendor elsewhere in the register, worth $70,420.12, and none of them were identified from it, because reference alone is not identification without amount corroboration (rule 12) and reference collisions between vendors are a documented trap. Third, on classification at scale: dominance over existing register rows was tested as the source of the account map and rejected. It returns Internal payroll allocation for 73313 Telecommunication Services and Internal workshop billing for 7B111 Internal Rates, because thin precedent carries noise. An explicit 45-account map was used instead, published on panel E with the precedent alongside and a Differs flag, and gated so every category it emits exists in Theme_Map and Theme_Map_v3.
- 93.0 v117 build (2-Sep-2026), the Harpley pull worklist | Two things are settled here. First, a pull list is a decision about where to stop, not a list of everything outstanding, so the sheet leads with the economics rather than the total: $227 a document on average, $180 median, $675 largest, and 82.6%% of the residual sits in bands B, C and D at under $500 a document. A worklist that does not say where to stop invites 1,094 sightings to move a figure that no reader of the register would notice. Second, the pull route follows from what is already held. The HAR073 APLEDGER creditor history is embedded and the ABN is proved, so identification is not the question and no Finance request is needed; every one of the 1,094 rows carries a TechOne attachment flag, so the route is attachment sighting via the Document Unique ID and the only thing gained is rule 17 line capture. Journal legs were held out of the list on the same logic: GJ066390 and GJ067382 are EOY 2022/23 AP reversals, and pulling an invoice would not resolve them because they rest on pairing, not on a document.
- 94.0 v118 build (2-Sep-2026), the promotion: FY2025/26 section-wide, FY2026/27 P1-3 both sections | Three things are settled. First, the scope decision: FY2025/26 is section-wide and in assessed scope from this build, on the financial analyst's direction that the year reads $8,559,879.90. That single decision is what made the load tractable. The v116 analysis found 6,943 of 7,189 analysis formulas keyed on financial year rather than on the scope column and concluded a retrofit was needed; with the year in scope those formulas are correct exactly as written, and the work collapsed to restating pinned constants and re-laying the three panels that carry static values or enumerate accounts. Second, the control that made the FY2026/27 load safe: every one of the 2,962 lines already loaded matched a line in the new pull on Document Unique ID, account, PK and amount, and the Park Services total agreed with SE2 to the cent before a row was written. Third, the asymmetry now carried (#415): two prior years at one service code against a section-wide FY2025/26. It is recorded on every panel that compares years and it is the reason #412 is the next acquisition.
- 95.0 v119 build (2-Sep-2026), Batch 58 (Binder1.pdf, Harpley Services Pty Ltd). 34 invoices, 92 printed lines, $117,985.39 ex GST, matched 1:1 to 34 register rows by Reference with the amount corroborated to the cent on every row (rule 12: reference alone is not identification). All 34 targets were AP lines carrying no Ev Invoice ID. Positions: EI data 5:3765 (totals 3767, controls 3778:3791); EIL 5:8951; recon 8955:12715 (3,761 rows); both citation sets rewritten from the EvID maps.
- 95.1 Duplicate screen (rule 12). Every corpus id screened prefix-aware against all 3,727 Evidence_Invoices ids across the 52 prefixes on the sheet. Five apparent hits (14725, QP-14746, QP-14767, 14928, 15150) are all Q Power (Qld) Pty Ltd, ABN 82 067 507 591 - the leading-zero false-match trap. Vendor-aware screening clears them; no invoice in this batch is a re-sighting. Harpley's EvID convention is INV- plus the full eight-digit reference.
- 95.2 Two printed templates, and the template-B GST-inclusive trap. Template A (QUANTITY / DESCRIPTION / UNIT PRICE(ex-GST) / TOTAL PRICE(ex-GST)) prints line amounts ex GST: 31 invoices, check 1 unvaried. Template B (letter form, DESCRIPTION / AMOUNT, no quantity column, no PK on the face) prints line amounts GST-INCLUSIVE: 00014350, 00015150 and 00015149, which carry the ratified check-1 inclB variant and lift GSTINC_ALLOWANCE by $5,325.56. The v3 corpus had divided those amounts by 1.1 and stored the quotient as line_ex_gst; a derived figure was replaced by the printed one (rule 19.2).
- 95.3 F1 printed-GST repair. The corpus carried printed_gst null on 33 of 34 documents. Every invoice prints the figure: three on a labelled "GST:" totals line, 31 as an unlabelled figure on its own line between "Sub Total:" and "Total Inc GST:" (precedent INV-00013912, register row 1306). Each was restated from the retained page text, and on all 34 the printed GST is exactly 10% of the printed subtotal, so check 3 is unvaried throughout.
- 95.4 Driver extension (rule 20). pswp_build_batch.py gains three mechanics, all shipped: (a) analysis_writes {row: {col: value}} for literal analysis-axis cells, restricted to an allow-list of columns EB/EC/Line Kind/Line Kind rule/Charge Source/Treatment/Procurement route and refusing to overwrite a formula, used here to move the v3 category off the "No invoice sighted" evidence state on all 34 rows; (b) evidence_supersede, which clears a named pre-capture placeholder from Evidence column AB before the sighting citation is appended, so a Confirmed row cannot read "not yet sighted" and "Sighted tax invoice" at once; (c) the documented v36 behaviour that the pre-capture follow-up is cleared on every green-blocked row unless the brief supplies one - the recreated copy of the driver had lost it. Verify audits all three.
- 95.5 Site attribution. All 34 sites are written from the printed Site Details, which outranks the charged PK: Hugh Muntz Park 25, Tully Memorial Park 8, Hugh Muntz Gardens 1, all three already on the Panel A gazetteer, basis "Sighted invoice site (printed)". The site and the PK contradict each other in both directions on four invoices (Open Item #419) and the printed PK differs from the charged PK on four (Open Item #418); neither affects capture, because every register amount ties its own printed subtotal.
- 96.0 v120 build (2-Sep-2026), Batch 59 (Tully.pdf, Harpley Services Pty Ltd). 19 pages: 14 unique invoices, 5 duplicate copies. 13 re-sightings screened (rule 12) and audited against the existing captures to the cent; never re-captured. One new capture, 00015218 ($90.00, FY2026/27 P1, register row 9115), Tier 1 on the printed ABN, Confirmed. Positions: EI data 5:3766 (totals 3768, controls 3779:3792); EIL 5:8952; recon 8956:12717 (3,762 rows); both citation sets rewritten from the EvID maps.
- 96.1 The re-sighting audit compares each screened corpus document to its existing Evidence_Invoices row on subtotal, GST, total and priced-line count, and to its Evidence_Invoice_Lines sum on the correct basis (printed subtotal, or printed Total Inc GST for the template-B 00015149). 13 of 13 agreed to the cent. The existing INV-00015203 capture carries a third zero-amount narrative row beyond its two priced lines, which is rule 16 line capture working as specified, not a discrepancy.
- 96.2 Reference collision surfaced by sighting (Open Item #422). Row 9115 carried a blanket evidence sentence tying its reference and amount to the KAC001 (Kachel) creditor ledger; the identical sentence sits on 78 FY2026/27 rows across at least four series. The sighted invoice proves Harpley on this row (rule 8: the printed ABN decides), the stamp is superseded here via evidence_supersede, and the remaining 77 rows are queued for a per-series restatement.
- 97.0 v121 build (2-Sep-2026), Batch 60 (PLA073 + LEV002 full creditor histories, 2-Sep-2026 pulls). Identification build under the v35 standing rules: reference match is accepted only with the transaction amount (incl GST) tying the register ex GST at 1.1 within 2c; document dates agree on all 1,094 identifications; Confirmed rows and rows carrying an Ev Invoice ID are never targets; column T untouched.
- 97.1 Population: the v118 FY2025/26 section-wide promotion and the FY2026/27 memo year. Play Force Australia Pty Ltd takes 928 previously Unidentified lines ($1,212,882.32) plus 5 series-inferred relabels; T & H Levai Pty Ltd takes 147 previously Unidentified lines ($492,512.55) plus 14 series-inferred relabels. 52 anchored-label rows (27 Play Force, 25 Levai) restate Tier 2 -> 1 on the same corroboration.
- 97.2 480 $0.00 companion rows identified via the same-document sibling rule (v35): each shares its reference AND TechOne Document Unique ID with an amount-corroborated non-zero sibling; identity columns only, no enquiry block, no green block. The 1,513 - 480 = 1,033 remaining zero rows matched by reference string alone (896 of them the FY2024/25 20392-scope standing-order companions whose valued legs post outside the loaded scope) stay Unidentified: amount corroboration cannot run on a zero and no loaded sibling exists.
- 97.3 Creditor_Lines +2,182 verbatim rows (PLA073 2,019; LEV002 163), matched-reference population per the sheet rule; CL_DATA restated 5:22937. LEV002 documents dated before 1-Jan-2023 whose reference strings collide with loaded register references (175 lines, same-vendor numeric reuse from the 2017-2022 era) are excluded as settled prior-era transactions, disclosed here and in Data_Acquisition. PLA073 export totals row ties to the cent (the v87 free control); LEV002 fails it by $9,900.00 (#424).
- 97.4 Held: 60 PLA073 reference matches fail the amount gate ($138,597.72) and take no identity (#423); the INV-2xxx FY2026/27 block among them remains the PAR/340C/2026 second vendor (#22). Zero conflict rows: no row carrying another vendor's label ties either history on amount.
- 97.5 Session scripts prepare_b121.py / b121_build.py (brief_b121.json, pins_b121.json); fold the enquiry-block, companion and Creditor_Lines mechanics into pswp_ident_batch.py when the original driver travels (the recreated skeleton is not extended in place).
- 98.0 v122 build (2-Sep-2026), Batch 61. Journal coverage build. The pull unit is the TechOne DOCUMENT FILE, not the journal reference: one Document Line Table export covers one document file, and a single document file can carry several references (document 1252466 carries eight IJ references and 2,020 register lines).
- 98.1 Rule 12 re-sighting audit on three of the six exports (1228733, 1228957, 1183397): each was compared leg by leg against the embedded copy on ledger, account, fund account, resource group, resource code, amount and narrative, and all three were line-identical. They were NOT re-captured. A re-pull that ties is corroboration of the embed, not new evidence.
- 98.2 Three new documents embedded verbatim with leg-level in-scope flags in column L: GJ079265 (1229435, 6 legs, Kachel INV 7530 re-split across PK000025 to PK000030, nets zero in scope), GJ080188 (1246521, 12 legs, plant hire billing PF to PK, one leg in scope at $27,374.51) and GJ080350 (1248576, 24 legs, a mixed recode journal, five legs in scope summing $27,374.51). Every document nets to exactly zero and every in-scope leg sum ties its own register lines to the cent.
- 98.3 Journal_Pull (new sheet) replaces Priority_Pull as the working queue and tiers the 310 outstanding documents by EVIDENCE ROUTE rather than by size. Tier A (46 documents, $2,482,242.39 absolute net) carries no TechOne attachment, so the Document Line Table is the only route. Tier B (176 documents) carries an attachment, so sighting it is cheaper and stronger than a pull. Tier C (88 documents) is the RJ reversing population, which nets to exactly zero and is read as a pair under rules 5 and 6. Ranking is by ABSOLUTE NET movement into scope, never gross: gross counts both legs of a transfer and would have put $29,818,798.82 of double-counted reversal traffic at the top of the queue.
