- 98.4 Priority_Pull is retained as the historical PK000022-scoped list and annotated per row with a v122 pull status limb (column K); 15 of its 64 references are already embedded. It is not deleted and not repurposed, so the earlier control still reads against its own population.
- 98.5 corpus_mix1.json was received, md5-logged and HELD. Its capture report records the OCR gate failing at 83 of 317 pages and 15 document runs capturing $0.00 against a printed total. Rule 19.2 admits no partial gate, so no line of it was captured at v122 (#427).
- 98.6 Session script b122_build.py. This build writes no Register cell: the control total is proven identical pre and post as a matter of arithmetic, not of intention.
- Session A (v123) - defect repair, no structural change and no rule amendment.
- Named ranges are now the addressing standard for the check-1 chain: EIL_Invoice and EIL_Amount cover Evidence_Invoice_Lines rows 5:17000, which is 8,048 rows beyond the current last data row 8,952. Appending line rows below 8,952 is now safe.
- The rule 17 check-1 per-invoice control panel lives on EIL_Controls, not on the data sheet. Column A holds the invoice id, B sums the captured lines over the named range, C reads the printed target from Evidence_Invoices by INDEX/MATCH, D records the basis (ex GST subtotal or, for 37 invoices, the printed incl-GST total under rule 16(b)) and E returns the literal TRUE.
- Freeze_Log column D stores the pre-freeze formula as TEXT. It is a record, not a calculation, and no longer reads Theme_Map.
- Register defined names: Reg_Data, Reg_Amount, Reg_FY, Reg_Site, Reg_EvID, all bounded at rows 5:31364. Every cross-sheet reference to columns T, E, DY and CJ used exactly these bounds before the change, so the substitution is value-neutral by construction.
- Controls sheet (v124). One row per control: group, sheet, what it proves, the live value, the expected value, and the result. Column E is always a reference or a count over a control that exists elsewhere, never a restatement, so the sheet cannot drift from what it reports.
- Master verdict Controls!B4 is TRUE only when no control reads FALSE AND the count of TRUE results equals the registered control count. A control that goes missing therefore fails the master cell just as loudly as one that reads FALSE.
- Error sweep (Controls group 6). SUMPRODUCT(--ISERROR(range)) over every formula-bearing sheet, and over the formula columns U, Z, DQ:DT, EA:EB, EI:EJ and EM:EN on the Register. Sheets holding no formulas are not swept and cannot generate an error value.
- Adding a control means adding a row to Controls and updating CONTROLS_COUNT in Config. Nothing else changes.
- 15.0 Identification from creditor histories (v125). A register line is cleared from Unidentified only where the APLEDGER creditor history carries the same reference, the history amount incl GST equals the register amount ex GST at 1.1 within 2 cents, and the document dates agree. Where the reference resolves to more than one creditor account on either the new histories or the lines already held, the row is left Unidentified: reference alone is not identification (rule 12).
- Zero-amount companion rows are never identified from a reference match alone. They carry no amount to corroborate against, so a reference hit is a matching aid, not evidence (rule 19.9).
- 14.0 Batches 11 and 112: rule 16 line capture and rule 17 register capture on 48 documents, all matched one to one against AP rows by reference and printed subtotal.

## 6. What this reconstruction does not carry

- **Per-build positions for v10 to v122.** The lean schema says the original held them. Config
  holds only the CURRENT positions, so the historical ones are unrecoverable from the workbook.
  The v14 positions quoted in the old lean schema (`REGISTER_DATA 5:19232`, `CONTROL_TOTAL
  10677585.33`) are the only historical set that survived, and they survived by being stale.
- **The reasoning behind settled decisions**, except where a build wrote it into Method, a
  Vendor_Series footnote or the Handover sheet.
- **v123, v124, v125 and v126 build detail**, which lives in the lean schema sections 11 to 14
  rather than here, because those builds are recent enough to be working knowledge.
